## assistant — 2026-05-15T00:30:47.346Z

I found a real bug. The Adrianne service files have broken `Environment=` lines — systemd is rejecting them because the values contain spaces and aren't quoted:

- `Environment=FUB_FROM_PHONE=(725) 777-1352` → systemd splits on the space, drops `777-1352`
- `Environment=SENDGRID_FROM_NAME=Adrianne Dunlap` → splits on space, drops `Dunlap`

Net effect: Adrianne's text/email runs have been firing without her phone-number/name overrides, falling back to `.env` defaults (likely Matthew's). Let me verify and fix.

## assistant — 2026-05-15T00:34:46.327Z

All good. Here's the rundown:

## What I found and fixed

**Your active folder IS the latest** — actually slightly newer than the migration zip. The only file that differs is `send_due_texts.py`, where the active version (May 14 16:49) has a fix: instead of skipping messages that contain the dead `matthew.thevegasagent.com` URL, it replaces them with the contact's `customSierraLoginURL` from FUB. That's an upgrade over the migration version.

**Real bug I caught and fixed:** Your two Adrianne systemd services had broken `Environment=` lines. systemd splits unquoted values on whitespace, so:
- `Environment=FUB_FROM_PHONE=(725) 777-1352` → systemd dropped `777-1352`
- `Environment=SENDGRID_FROM_NAME=Adrianne Dunlap` → systemd dropped `Dunlap`

Net effect: Adrianne's scheduled runs would have fallen back to `.env` defaults (your phone and the joint sender name). I quoted both lines and reloaded the daemon. Verified systemd now sees the full values.

Good news on timing: those broken service files were just created today around 17:14, and **Adrianne's first scheduled Linux run hasn't happened yet** (next fire: tomorrow 08:30). No texts went out under the broken config.

## Tonight's schedule

All 4 timers armed correctly:
- 23:00 — nightly email queue + morning text queue (Matthew)
- 23:30 — same for Adrianne
- Tomorrow 08:00 / 08:30 — first text runs (now with correct Adrianne phone)

## Optional cleanup (nothing urgent)

A few odd files I noticed in `/home/matthew/automation/` — leaving them alone unless you say otherwise:
- `logs/text_runner_%Y-%m-%d.log` — artifact from an earlier service file that didn't escape `%Y` properly. Current services use `%%Y`. Safe to delete.
- `ystemctl --user daemon-reload` — looks like a shell command that got captured as a filename (missing leading `s`). Safe to delete.
- `send_due_texts.py.bak` — backup of the pre-fix script. Safe to keep or delete.

Enjoy your time away. The 8am Adrianne run will now go out from her number.

## assistant — 2026-05-15T00:40:54.431Z

Both Chromes are running on 9222/9223. Good. But here are the gaps for staying running while you're out:

1. **No self-healing on Linux yet** — your Windows .bats auto-launch Chrome if it's down; the Linux systemd services don't. If Chrome crashes or the FUB session times out, the next text run silently fails.
2. **No Chrome autostart** — if the laptop reboots, neither Chrome comes back.
3. **`launch_chrome_debug.sh` in the repo is wrong** — it uses the wrong profile path (`$HOME/chrome-debug`), not the `.chrome-debug-matthew` / `.chrome-debug-adrianne` that are actually running. If anything ever called it, it'd launch a fresh profile with no FUB login.

Let me fix all three.

{
  "file_path": "/home/matthew/automation/ensure_chrome.sh",
  "content": "#!/bin/bash\n# ensure_chrome.sh <port> <profile-dir> <label>\n# Self-heal: if Chrome debug isn't responding on <port>, launch it with <profile-dir>.\n# Used as ExecStartPre by the fub-* systemd services so a crashed Chrome\n# doesn't silently break the next scheduled run.\n\nset -u\nPORT=\"${1:?usage: ensure_chrome.sh <port> <profile-dir> <label>}\"\nPROFILE=\"${2:?missing profile dir}\"\nLABEL=\"${3:-chrome}\"\n\nif curl -fsS --max-time 2 \"http://localhost:${PORT}/json/version\" > /dev/null 2>&1; then\n    exit 0\nfi\n\necho \"$(date -Is) ensure_chrome[$LABEL]: port ${PORT} unreachable, launching\"\nexport DISPLAY=\"${DISPLAY:-:0}\"\nexport XAUTHORITY=\"${XAUTHORITY:-/home/matthew/.Xauthority}\"\n\nnohup /usr/bin/google-chrome \\\n    --remote-debugging-port=\"${PORT}\" \\\n    --remote-allow-origins=* \\\n    --user-data-dir=\"${PROFILE}\" \\\n    \"https://teamdunlaprealty.followupboss.com\" \\\n    >> \"/tmp/ensure_chrome_${LABEL}.log\" 2>&1 &\n\nfor i in $(seq 1 20); do\n    sleep 1\n    if curl -fsS --max-time 2 \"http://localhost:${PORT}/json/version\" > /dev/null 2>&1; then\n        echo \"$(date -Is) ensure_chrome[$LABEL]: up after ${i}s\"\n        exit 0\n    fi\ndone\n\necho \"$(date -Is) ensure_chrome[$LABEL]: FAILED to come up on port ${PORT}\"\nexit 1\n"
}
```

128	## 5. Known kinks / things to watch
129	
130	### Resolved today
131	- ✅ Triple-tap bug in old `run_send_ai_texts_LIVE.bat` (three back-to-back passes wrote shadow tags 30 min apart, so d3/d5/d7 fired same morning). Rename + script archive prevents recurrence.
132	- ✅ Tripled Sierra API key via paste (`Ctrl+V` fired 3x). Single-paste verification in scripts now.
133	- ✅ FUB pagination crash at offset=2000 (FUB's hard cap). All three fetch functions now break gracefully.
134	- ✅ Cloud Run org policy override (`iam.allowedPolicyMemberDomains`). Allows `allUsers` to invoke service.
135	- ✅ Cloud Build IAM permission. Compute service account granted `cloudbuild.builds.builder`.
136	
137	### Active concerns
138	- **Laptop dependency:** Live-fire SPRIORITY texts require debug Chrome running with FUB logged in. If laptop sleeps mid-day or session expires, scheduled tasks fail. Mitigation: leave laptop plugged in, lid open. Long-term fix: Claude API migration (Phase 8).
139	- **Dunlap 4 Month Email Re Engage automation:** Still running on 8,339 enrolled contacts, despite "OFF" toggle. Decision: let it ride (Matthew said "leave it"). Risk: occasional double-email when our AI sends + Dunlap also sends.
140	- **FUB session expiry:** Cookies expire every few weeks. `verify_fub_session.py` checks before nightly, sends alert email if dead. Watch for alerts.
141	- **50 scheduled text cap (FUB):** We pre-queue 40 texts/morning. If anything else tries to schedule, we'd hit cap. Keep an eye on this.
142	- **2000 pagination cap (FUB):** For tags with 2000+ contacts (like 2026 Daily Search Done), we only see the first 2000. For 40 sends/day this is fine. If we ever need to reach the deeper contacts, need cursor pagination or split by sub-tag.
143	
144	---
145	
146	## 6. Critical credentials
147	
148	All live in `.env` in the project folder. **Never commit, never share.**
149	- `FUB_API_KEY` — Follow Up Boss API key (account-owner level)
150	- `SIERRA_API_KEY` — Sierra Interactive Direct API key (36-char UUID — single, NOT tripled)
151	- `SENDGRID_API_KEY` — outbound email (if/when used)
152	- `FUB_FROM_PHONE` — `(725) 600-6779`
153	
154	GCP credentials live in Secret Manager (no `.env` needed for Cloud Run service).
155	
156	---
157	
158	## 7. DNS / domain access
159	
160	- **thevegasagent.com** — managed by **Sierra Interactive** on Cloudflare. For DNS changes, email `support@sierrainteractive.com`. Matthew has no direct Cloudflare login.
161	- **teamdunlaprealty.com** — managed in Squarespace, Matthew's own login.
162	- **DEAD URL:** `matthew.thevegasagent.com` (old Ylopo CNAME shut down 2026-05-11). All send scripts now filter this URL from outgoing messages.
163	
164	---
165	
166	## 8. Pending work (roadmap, ordered by ROI)
167	
168	1. **Timezone-aware morning text queue** (~30 min) — pre-queue each text for 8am LOCAL time of the lead. ET leads at 8am ET, PT at 8am PT.
169	2. **Reply Intent Classifier (Phase 6, the "moneymaker")** — FUB webhook on inbound text/email → Claude API classifies intent → if hot, auto-tags "Call Today" → shows in smart list for power dialer. Estimated 1 weekend.
170	3. **Adrianne (wife) user split** — Each gets independent leads to call. Separate Chrome profile, separate FUB user, separate phone (Adrianne already has hers). Avoid duplicate effort. Estimated 1 day.
171	4. **FUB Smart List integration** — replace `--tag X` with `--smart-list-id N` so Matthew can change filtering criteria in FUB UI without code changes. Estimated 2 hours.
172	5. **Notes from FUB → Sierra sync** — Matthew currently does manually. Need bidirectional sync for notes/activity.
173	6. **Stage matching between Sierra and FUB** — currently divergent. Automate stage sync via both Sierra automations and FUB automations.
174	7. **Claude API migration** — kill FUB internal AI generator dependency. ~3 days work. Unlocks fully cloud-hosted everything.
175	8. **Property data enrichment** (Phase 8) — Estated / ATTOM / BatchData for equity/life-event signals. Felix-style reactive intelligence. Lower priority.
176	9. **Long-term Linux Mint laptop migration** — replace Windows laptop as home automation server. Or skip directly to cloud-only.
177	
178	---
179	
180	## 9. Vendor / product reference
181	
182	- **Sierra Interactive** — IDX/CRM, hosts thevegasagent.com Sierra site. Manages Matthew's Cloudflare DNS. Webhooks support `/webhook` endpoint (singular, no `s`). Sierra API requires `Sierra-ApiKey` + `Sierra-OriginatingSystemName` headers. Pagination uses `pageNumber`, NOT `page`.
183	- **Follow Up Boss** — CRM, source of truth for contacts. Public API at `api.followupboss.com/v1`. Internal SPA at `teamdunlaprealty.followupboss.com/api/v1` (needs cookies). Action plans + Automations (different features). 50-text scheduled cap. 2000-offset pagination cap.
184	- **Ed Neuhaus's free FUB MCP** — github.com/mindwear-capitian/followupboss-mcp-server. 152 tools. Public REST API only. Cowork waitlist for hosted version.
185	- **Mod AI (Mark Jensen)** — paid AI calling product. NOT free MCP (separate from Ed Neuhaus's). Matthew used to subscribe.
186	- **Fello / Felix** — competitor product. Inspiration for the Reply Intent Classifier pattern.
187	- **Ylopo** — old marketing platform, ruined Matthew's business previously. Avoid. Their CNAME (`matthew.thevegasagent.com`) shut down 2026-05-11.
188	- **HiFello (Higher Fello)** — referenced but not currently integrated.
189

194	1. Notes from FUB → Sierra sync
195	2. FUB ↔ Sierra stage matching automation
196	3. Adrianne's setup as separate user

199	Plus whatever Claude has worked on overnight (status snapshot, best practices doc, migration package, possibly more research).

203	## 11. How to find errors quickly
204	
205	| Symptom | Where to look |
206	|---|---|
207	| Tag added in Sierra but not in FUB | Cloud Run logs: `gcloud run services logs read sierra-fub-tag-pusher --region us-west1 --limit 30` |
208	| Morning texts didn't fire | `logs/queue_2026_texts_YYYY-MM-DD.log` |
209	| Nightly email run failed | `logs/queue_YYYY-MM-DD.log` |
210	| Live text runs failed | `logs/text_runner_YYYY-MM-DD.log` |
211	| AI generation failures | Look for "FAIL gen" in same logs |
212	| FUB session expired | Email alert from `notify_session_problem.py` to matthew@teamdunlaprealty.com |
213	| Sierra webhook stopped | `curl "https://api.sierrainteractivedev.com/webhook" -H "Sierra-ApiKey: $SIERRA_API_KEY" -H "Sierra-OriginatingSystemName: FUB-AutoLogin-Sync"` |
214	
215	---
216	
217	## 12. Why this exists (the human context)
218	
219	Matthew is rebuilding a real estate business that Ylopo broke. The automation is meant to:
220	- Surface 5-7 PRE-QUALIFIED conversations per day (not 100 cold calls)
221	- Free Tue/Thu for YouTube content
222	- Cut emotional labor of dealing with low-intent leads
223	- Get him and Adrianne to 4 sales/month combined → $500-750K/year
224	- Buy a second home in Ohio
225	
226	**The Reply Intent Classifier is the linchpin.** Everything else feeds it.

  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"One-off SPRIORITY rebalance: split ~50/50 between Matthew (uid=1) and\nAdrianne (uid=2), but never move a lead who has ever sent an inbound text.\n\nUsage:\n    python3 rebalance_spriority.py            # dry-run (prints plan, no changes)\n    python3 rebalance_spriority.py --live     # apply the reassignments\n\"\"\"\nimport os, sys, time\nfrom pathlib import Path\nimport requests\n\nfor line in (Path(__file__).parent / '.env').read_text().splitlines():\n    if line.strip() and not line.startswith('#') and '=' in line:\n        k, _, v = line.partition('=')\n        os.environ.setdefault(k.strip(), v.strip())\n\nKEY = os.environ['FUB_API_KEY']\nAUTH = (KEY, '')\nHDRS = {'X-System': 'TeamDunlap-LocalScript'}\nBASE = 'https://api.followupboss.com/v1'\n\nDRY = '--live' not in sys.argv\nUSER_M, USER_A = 1, 2\n\n\ndef fetch_spriority():\n    out, offset = [], 0\n    while True:\n        r = requests.get(f'{BASE}/people',\n                         params={'tags': 'SPRIORITY', 'limit': 100,\n                                 'offset': offset, 'fields': 'allFields'},\n                         auth=AUTH, headers=HDRS, timeout=30)\n        r.raise_for_status()\n        ppl = r.json().get('people', [])\n        if not ppl:\n            break\n        out.extend(ppl)\n        if len(ppl) < 100:\n            break\n        offset += 100\n        if offset >= 2000:\n            break\n    return out\n\n\ndef has_inbound(pid):\n    r = requests.get(f'{BASE}/textMessages',\n                     params={'personId': pid, 'limit': 100},\n                     auth=AUTH, headers=HDRS, timeout=30)\n    if r.status_code != 200:\n        return False\n    return any(m.get('isIncoming') for m in r.json().get('textmessages', []))\n\n\ndef reassign(pid, new_uid):\n    r = requests.put(f'{BASE}/people/{pid}',\n                     json={'assignedUserId': new_uid},\n                     auth=AUTH, headers=HDRS, timeout=30)\n    return r.status_code, r.text[:200]\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(f'Mode: {\"DRY-RUN\" if DRY else \"LIVE\"}')\n    print('Fetching SPRIORITY contacts...')\n    ppl = fetch_spriority()\n    print(f'  {len(ppl)} contacts')\n\n    m_eligible, m_responder, a_existing, other = [], [], [], []\n    print('Scanning inbound text status (one API call per contact)...')\n    for i, p in enumerate(ppl, 1):\n        pid, name, uid = p['id'], p.get('name', '?'), p.get('assignedUserId')\n        if uid == USER_M:\n            if has_inbound(pid):\n                m_responder.append((pid, name))\n            else:\n                m_eligible.append((pid, name))\n        elif uid == USER_A:\n            a_existing.append((pid, name))\n        else:\n            other.append((pid, name, uid))\n        if i % 25 == 0:\n            print(f'  [{i}/{len(ppl)}] scanned')\n        time.sleep(0.05)\n\n    print()\n    print(f'Matthew, no inbound (movable):  {len(m_eligible)}')\n    print(f'Matthew, has replied (KEEP):    {len(m_responder)}')\n    print(f'Adrianne, already hers:         {len(a_existing)}')\n    print(f'Other assignment:               {len(other)}')\n\n    total_pool = len(m_eligible) + len(a_existing)\n    target_each = total_pool // 2\n    to_move = max(0, target_each - len(a_existing))\n\n    print()\n    print(f'Non-responder pool: {total_pool}  |  Target each: {target_each}'\n          f'  |  Move {to_move} from Matthew -> Adrianne')\n\n    m_eligible.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])\n    movers = m_eligible[::2][:to_move]\n    if len(movers) < to_move:\n        remaining = [x for x in m_eligible if x not in movers]\n        movers += remaining[:to_move - len(movers)]\n\n    print(f'Selected {len(movers)} movers. First 10:')\n    for pid, name in movers[:10]:\n        print(f'    {pid:>6}  {name}')\n    if len(movers) > 10:\n        print(f'    ... and {len(movers) - 10} more')\n\n    if DRY:\n        print('\\nDRY-RUN complete. No changes made. Re-run with --live to apply.')\n        return\n\n    print('\\nApplying reassignments...')\n    ok = fail = 0\n    for i, (pid, name) in enumerate(movers, 1):\n        code, body = reassign(pid, USER_A)\n        if code == 200:\n            ok += 1\n        else:\n            fail += 1\n            print(f'  FAIL {pid} {name}: HTTP {code} {body}')\n        if i % 10 == 0:\n            print(f'  [{i}/{len(movers)}] ok={ok} fail={fail}')\n        time.sleep(0.1)\n\n    print(f'\\nDone: reassigned={ok} failed={fail} skipped_responders={len(m_responder)}')\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n"
}

"""
send_d1_emails_now.py — fire d1 emails IMMEDIATELY via SendGrid (not scheduled
for tomorrow morning). Writes shadow tags so the nightly task skips these
leads and picks fresh ones.

Run twice tonight (once Matthew, once Adrianne via env override) to spread
half the volume immediately and let the 11pm nightly handle the rest.

Filters: tag presence, stage exclusions, contact delay tags, dead URL filter.

Usage (Matthew, ~7pm):
  python send_d1_emails_now.py --tag SPRIORITY --cap 50
  python send_d1_emails_now.py --tag "2026 Daily Search Done" --skip-if-also-tagged SPRIORITY --cap 250

Usage (Adrianne, parallel — set env first):

  set SENDGRID_FROM_NAME=Adrianne Dunlap
  set SENDGRID_REPLY_TO=adrianneldunlap@gmail.com
  set FUB_DEBUG_PORT=9223
  python send_d1_emails_now.py --tag "2026 Daily Search Done" --skip-if-also-tagged SPRIORITY --cap 250 --skip 250
"""
import os
import sys
import time
import json
import argparse
import requests
from pathlib import Path

try:
    sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
except Exception:
    pass


def safe_print(s):
    try:
        print(s, flush=True)
    except UnicodeEncodeError:
        print(str(s).encode("ascii", "replace").decode("ascii"), flush=True)

}

"""
Diagnostic probe to find the right Sierra endpoint for fetching a single
lead's full details, including the auto-login URL.

Tries several URL patterns and HTTP methods, dumps every field on the
lead so we can spot the auto-login URL no matter what it's called.
"""

import os
import json
import requests
from pathlib import Path


def load_env():
    env_path = Path(__file__).parent / ".env"
    for line in env_path.read_text().splitlines():
        line = line.strip()
        if not line or line.startswith("#") or "=" not in line:
            continue
        key, _, value = line.partition("=")
        os.environ[key.strip()] = value.strip().strip('"').strip("'")


load_env()

HEADERS = {
    "Sierra-ApiKey": os.environ["SIERRA_API_KEY"],
    "Sierra-OriginatingSystemName": os.environ.get(
        "SIERRA_ORIGINATING_SYSTEM", "FUB-AutoLogin-Sync"
    ),
    "Content-Type": "application/json",
}
BASE = "https://api.sierrainteractivedev.com"


def hr(label):
    print("\n" + "=" * 70)
    print(label)
    print("=" * 70)


# -------- Step 1: pull one lead from /leads/find and dump all its fields --------
hr("STEP 1 — Listing fields on a lead from /leads/find")
r = requests.get(f"{BASE}/leads/find", headers=HEADERS,
                 params={"page": 1, "pageSize": 1}, timeout=15)
print(f"Status: {r.status_code}")
data = r.json().get("data", {})
leads = data.get("leads", [])
if not leads:
    print("No leads returned, stopping.")
    raise SystemExit
lead = leads[0]
lead_id = lead.get("id")
print(f"Sample lead id: {lead_id}")
print(f"All fields on this lead (find listing):")
for k in sorted(lead.keys()):
    v = lead[k]
    if isinstance(v, str) and len(v) > 60:
        v = v[:60] + "..."
```

{

  "description": "Probe FUB for matching tag names"
}

{
  "command": "cd ~/automation && python3 -c \"\nimport requests, os, json\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom collections import Counter\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta\nfor line in (Path('.')/ '.env').read_text().splitlines():\n    if line.strip() and not line.startswith('#') and '=' in line:\n        k,_,v = line.partition('=')\n        os.environ.setdefault(k.strip(), v.strip())\nkey = os.environ['FUB_API_KEY']\n\n# Pull 500 most-recent outbound calls and tally outcomes\ncalls = []\noffset = 0\nwhile offset < 1000:\n    r = requests.get('https://api.followupboss.com/v1/calls', params={'limit':100,'offset':offset,'sort':'-created'}, auth=(key,''))\n    if r.status_code != 200: print('http',r.status_code); break\n    batch = r.json().get('calls',[])\n    if not batch: break\n    calls.extend(batch)\n    offset += 100\n\n# Outbound only\nout = [c for c in calls if not c.get('isIncoming')]\nprint(f'Pulled {len(calls)} calls ({len(out)} outbound).  Earliest in pull: {calls[-1].get(\\\"startedAt\\\") if calls else \\\"?\\\"}.')\n\noutcomes = Counter(c.get('outcome') for c in out)\nprint()\nprint('Outcome distribution (last ~1000 outbound calls):')\nfor o, n in outcomes.most_common():\n    print(f'  {n:>5}  {o!r}')\n\n# Define ANSWERED as duration > 0 and outcome NOT in non-pickup classes\nnonpickup = {'No Answer','Left Voicemail','Bad Number','Wrong Number','Disconnected','Busy',None}\nanswered_count = sum(1 for c in out if c.get('duration',0) > 30 and c.get('outcome') not in nonpickup)\nprint(f'\\\\nAnswered (duration > 30s AND outcome != non-pickup): {answered_count}/{len(out)} = {100*answered_count/max(1,len(out)):.1f}%')\n\" 2>&1",
  "description": "Pull recent FUB calls and tally outcomes"
}
```

{
  "command": "cd ~/automation && python3 -c \"\nimport requests, os\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom collections import Counter\nfor line in (Path('.')/ '.env').read_text().splitlines():\n    if line.strip() and not line.startswith('#') and '=' in line:\n        k,_,v = line.partition('=')\n        os.environ.setdefault(k.strip(), v.strip())\nkey = os.environ['FUB_API_KEY']\n\ncalls = []\noffset = 0\nwhile offset < 2000:\n    r = requests.get('https://api.followupboss.com/v1/calls', params={'limit':100,'offset':offset,'sort':'-created'}, auth=(key,''))\n    batch = r.json().get('calls',[])\n    if not batch: break\n    calls.extend(batch)\n    offset += 100\n\nout = [c for c in calls if not c.get('isIncoming')]\nprint(f'Pulled {len(out)} outbound calls. Date range: {out[-1].get(\\\"startedAt\\\")} -> {out[0].get(\\\"startedAt\\\")}')\n\n# Duration histogram\ndur_buckets = Counter()\nfor c in out:\n    d = c.get('duration') or 0\n    if d == 0: dur_buckets['0s'] += 1\n    elif d < 10: dur_buckets['1-9s'] += 1\n    elif d < 30: dur_buckets['10-29s'] += 1\n    elif d < 60: dur_buckets['30-59s'] += 1\n    elif d < 120: dur_buckets['60-119s'] += 1\n    elif d < 300: dur_buckets['2-5min'] += 1\n    else: dur_buckets['5min+'] += 1\nprint('Duration distribution:')\nfor k in ['0s','1-9s','10-29s','30-59s','60-119s','2-5min','5min+']:\n    print(f'  {k:<10}  {dur_buckets[k]}')\n\n# Outcome \u00d7 duration cross-tab\nprint()\nprint('Outcome x duration > 30s:')\nct = Counter()\nfor c in out:\n    o = c.get('outcome') or 'None/untagged'\n    d = c.get('duration') or 0\n    ct[(o, d > 30)] += 1\nfor (o,gt30), n in sorted(ct.items(), key=lambda x: -x[1]):\n    print(f'  outcome={o!r:<22} duration>30s={gt30}  n={n}')\n\" 2>&1",
  "description": "Deeper call duration / outcome analysis"
}

{
  "command": "cd ~/automation && python3 -c \"\nimport requests, os, json\nfrom pathlib import Path\nimport time\nfor line in (Path('.')/ '.env').read_text().splitlines():\n    if line.strip() and not line.startswith('#') and '=' in line:\n        k,_,v = line.partition('=')\n        os.environ.setdefault(k.strip(), v.strip())\nH = {'Sierra-ApiKey': os.environ['SIERRA_API_KEY'], 'Sierra-OriginatingSystemName':'FUB-AutoLogin-Sync'}\nBASE = 'https://api.sierrainteractivedev.com'\n\n# Pull all Sierra leads, collect (email, visits, id, name, status, updateDate)\nall_leads = []\npage = 1\nwhile True:\n    r = requests.get(f'{BASE}/leads/find', headers=H, params={'pageSize':100,'pageNumber':page,'page':page}, timeout=30)\n    if r.status_code != 200:\n        print(f'page {page} HTTP {r.status_code}'); break\n    leads = r.json().get('data',{}).get('leads',[])\n    if not leads: break\n    for L in leads:\n        all_leads.append({\n            'id': L.get('id'),\n            'email': (L.get('email') or '').lower(),\n            'visits': L.get('visits',0) or 0,\n            'first': L.get('firstName',''),\n            'last': L.get('lastName',''),\n            'status': L.get('leadStatus',''),\n            'updated': L.get('updateDate',''),\n        })\n    print(f'  page {page}: cum={len(all_leads)}')\n    page += 1\n    if page > 110: break\n    time.sleep(0.15)\n\n# Save\nwith open('sierra_leads_2026-05-14.json','w') as f:\n    json.dump(all_leads, f)\n\n# Stats\nprint(f'\\\\nTotal Sierra leads pulled: {len(all_leads)}')\nfrom collections import Counter\nbuckets = Counter()\nfor L in all_leads:\n    v = L['visits']\n    if v == 0: buckets['0'] += 1\n    elif v < 3: buckets['1-2'] += 1\n    elif v < 6: buckets['3-5'] += 1\n    elif v < 11: buckets['6-10'] += 1\n    elif v < 21: buckets['11-20'] += 1\n    else: buckets['21+'] += 1\nprint('Visits distribution:')\nfor k in ['0','1-2','3-5','6-10','11-20','21+']:\n    print(f'  {k:<6}  {buckets[k]}')\n\n# Top 30 by visits with an email\ntop = sorted([L for L in all_leads if L['visits'] >= 5 and L['email']], key=lambda x: -x['visits'])[:30]\nprint('\\\\nTop 30 by visits (with email):')\nfor L in top:\n    print(f'  visits={L[\\\"visits\\\"]:>4}  {L[\\\"first\\\"]} {L[\\\"last\\\"]:<25}  status={L[\\\"status\\\"]:<12}  email={L[\\\"email\\\"]}')\n\" 2>&1 | tail -80",
  "description": "Pull all Sierra leads with visit counts",
  "run_in_background": true
}

Matthew=7256006779, Adrianne=7257771352\nall_msgs = []\nfor label, num in [('matthew','7256006779'),('adrianne','7257771352')]:\n    print(f'=== {label} {num} ===')\n    offset = 0\n    while offset < 5000:\n        r = requests.get('https://api.followupboss.com/v1/textMessages', params={'fromNumber':num,'limit':100,'offset':offset,'sort':'-created'}, auth=(key,''))\n        if r.status_code != 200:\n            print(f'  HTTP {r.status_code} at offset {offset}: {r.text[:200]}'); break\n        batch = r.json().get('textmessages',[])\n        if not batch: break\n        all_msgs.extend(batch)\n        offset += 100\n        time.sleep(0.15)\n    print(f'  pulled {offset}')\n\nout = []\nfor m in all_msgs:\n    body = m.get('message','')\n    if not body or '* Body is hidden' in body: continue\n    out.append({\n        'id': m.get('id'),\n        'personId': m.get('personId'),\n        'created': m.get('created'),\n        'userName': m.get('userName'),\n        'fromNumber': m.get('fromNumber'),\n        'message': body,\n        'isIncoming': m.get('isIncoming'),\n        'deliveryStatus': m.get('deliveryStatus'),\n    })\n\nwith open('outbound_text_corpus_2026-05-14.json','w') as f:\n    json.dump(out, f, indent=2)\n\nprint(f'\\\\nSaved {len(out)} messages with visible bodies.')\nfrom collections import Counter\nprint('By sender:')\nfor u,n in Counter((m['userName'],m['fromNumber']) for m in out).most_common(10):\n    print(f'  {u}: {n}')\nprint()\nprint('First 3 sample bodies:')\nfor m in out[:3]:\n    print(f'  --- {m[\\\"created\\\"]} from {m[\\\"userName\\\"]} ---')\n    print(f'  {m[\\\"message\\\"][:200]}')\n\" 2>&1 | tail -25",
  "description": "Pull text corpus by fromNumber",
  "run_in_background": true
}

```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/matthew/automation/analyze_engagement.py",
  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Cross-reference Sierra visit counts with FUB inbound-text history\nto surface two action lists:\n\n  1. HIGH_VISIT_REENGAGE_2026-05-14 \u2014 leads with many Sierra visits but no\n     inbound text response. Email them tonight (re-engagement push).\n\n  2. CALL_TODAY_2026-05-15 \u2014 leads ranked by engagement signal (visits +\n     inbound text recency + tags) for tomorrow's dialer. Target 25-50.\n\nOutputs:\n  - high_visit_reengage_2026-05-14.txt    (list of FUB person IDs + names)\n  - call_today_2026-05-15.txt              (ranked call list)\n  - Adds the corresponding tags to FUB in --live mode.\n\"\"\"\nimport os, sys, json, time\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom collections import Counter\nimport requests\n\nfor line in (Path(__file__).parent / '.env').read_text().splitlines():\n    if line.strip() and not line.startswith('#') and '=' in line:\n        k, _, v = line.partition('=')\n        os.environ.setdefault(k.strip(), v.strip())\n\nKEY = os.environ['FUB_API_KEY']\nAUTH = (KEY, '')\nHDRS = {'X-System': 'TeamDunlap-LocalScript'}\nBASE = 'https://api.followupboss.com/v1'\n\nLIVE = '--live' in sys.argv\n\nREENGAGE_TAG = 'HIGH_VISIT_REENGAGE_2026-05-14'\nCALL_TODAY_TAG = 'CALL_TODAY_2026-05-15'\n\n# Tuning thresholds\nHIGH_VISIT_THRESHOLD = 5   # 5+ Sierra visits = \"engaged with site\"\nCALL_LIST_TOP_N = 50       # how many leads on tomorrow's call list\n\n\ndef has_inbound_text(pid):\n    \"\"\"True if this FUB person has ever sent us an inbound text.\"\"\"\n    r = requests.get(f'{BASE}/textMessages',\n                     params={'personId': pid, 'limit': 100},\n                     auth=AUTH, headers=HDRS, timeout=30)\n    if r.status_code != 200:\n        return None  # unknown\n    return any(m.get('isIncoming') for m in r.json().get('textmessages', []))\n\n\ndef fub_find_by_email(email):\n    r = requests.get(f'{BASE}/people',\n                     params={'email': email, 'limit': 1, 'fields': 'allFields'},\n                     auth=AUTH, headers=HDRS, timeout=30)\n    if r.status_code != 200:\n        return None\n    ppl = r.json().get('people', [])\n    return ppl[0] if ppl else None\n\n\ndef tag_person(pid, tag):\n    \"\"\"Add a tag by reading current tags and PUT-ing the new full list (FUB pattern).\"\"\"\n    r = requests.get(f'{BASE}/people/{pid}', params={'fields': 'allFields'},\n                     auth=AUTH, headers=HDRS, timeout=30)\n    if r.status_code != 200:\n        return False, f'GET {r.status_code}'\n    current = r.json().get('tags', []) or []\n    if tag in current:\n        return True, 'already-tagged'\n    new = current + [tag]\n    pr = requests.put(f'{BASE}/people/{pid}', json={'tags': new},\n                      auth=AUTH, headers=HDRS, timeout=30)\n    return pr.status_code == 200, f'PUT {pr.status_code}'\n\n\ndef main():\n    sierra_path = Path('sierra_leads_2026-05-14.json')\n    if not sierra_path.exists():\n        print('Missing sierra_leads_2026-05-14.json \u2014 run the Sierra pull first.')\n        sys.exit(1)\n    sierra = json.loads(sierra_path.read_text())\n    print(f'Loaded {len(sierra)} Sierra leads.')\n\n    high_visit = [L for L in sierra if L['visits'] >= HIGH_VISIT_THRESHOLD and L['email']]\n    print(f'Sierra leads with visits >= {HIGH_VISIT_THRESHOLD} and email: {len(high_visit)}')\n\n    # Cross-reference each to FUB and gather signal\n    print('Cross-referencing to FUB...')\n    enriched = []\n    for i, L in enumerate(high_visit, 1):\n        fub = fub_find_by_email(L['email'])\n        if not fub:\n            continue\n        pid = fub['id']\n        tags = fub.get('tags', []) or []\n        # Skip opted-out\n        if any('opt' in t.lower() or 'unsubscribe' in t.lower() for t in tags):\n            continue\n        inbound = has_inbound_text(pid)\n        enriched.append({\n            'sierra_id': L['id'],\n            'fub_id': pid,\n            'name': fub.get('name', f\"{L['first']} {L['last']}\"),\n            'email': L['email'],\n            'visits': L['visits'],\n            'has_inbound': inbound,\n            'sierra_status': L['status'],\n            'fub_stage': fub.get('stage'),\n            'is_spriority': 'SPRIORITY' in tags,\n            'in_2026_pool': '2026 Daily Search Done' in tags,\n            'has_phone': bool(fub.get('phones') and any(p.get('value') for p in fub['phones'])),\n        })\n        if i % 25 == 0:\n            print(f'  {i}/{len(high_visit)}')\n        time.sleep(0.1)\n\n    print(f'Enriched: {len(enriched)} matched Sierra->FUB.')\n\n    # Bucket: high-visit-no-engagement (no inbound text)\n    reengage = [e for e in enriched if e['has_inbound'] is False]\n    reengage.sort(key=lambda x: -x['visits'])\n    print(f'\\\\n=== HIGH_VISIT_REENGAGE candidates: {len(reengage)} ===')\n    for e in reengage[:20]:\n        print(f\"  visits={e['visits']:>4}  {e['name']:<28}  spr={e['is_spriority']}  phone={e['has_phone']}  email={e['email']}\")\n    if len(reengage) > 20:\n        print(f'  ...and {len(reengage)-20} more')\n\n    # Call list: rank engaged ones for tomorrow\n    callable = [e for e in enriched if e['has_phone']]\n    for e in callable:\n        e['score'] = (\n            e['visits']\n            + (15 if e['has_inbound'] else 0)\n            + (20 if e['is_spriority'] else 0)\n            + (5 if e['in_2026_pool'] else 0)\n        )\n    callable.sort(key=lambda x: -x['score'])\n    call_today = callable[:CALL_LIST_TOP_N]\n    print(f'\\\\n=== CALL_TODAY top {CALL_LIST_TOP_N}: ===')\n    for e in call_today[:20]:\n        print(f\"  score={e['score']:>4}  visits={e['visits']:>3}  inbound={e['has_inbound']!s:<5}  spr={e['is_spriority']!s:<5}  {e['name']}\")\n\n    # Persist text reports\n    Path('high_visit_reengage_2026-05-14.txt').write_text(\n        '\\\\n'.join(f\"{e['fub_id']}\\\\t{e['name']}\\\\t{e['email']}\\\\tvisits={e['visits']}\"\n                  for e in reengage)\n    )\n    Path('call_today_2026-05-15.txt').write_text(\n        '\\\\n'.join(f\"{e['fub_id']}\\\\t{e['name']}\\\\t{e['email']}\\\\tscore={e['score']}\\\\tvisits={e['visits']}\\\\tinbound={e['has_inbound']}\\\\tspriority={e['is_spriority']}\"\n                  for e in call_today)\n    )\n\n    if not LIVE:\n        print('\\\\nDRY-RUN \u2014 no tags written. Re-run with --live to tag.')\n        return\n\n    print(f'\\\\nTagging {len(reengage)} contacts with {REENGAGE_TAG!r}...')\n    ok = fail = 0\n    for e in reengage:\n        s, why = tag_person(e['fub_id'], REENGAGE_TAG)\n        if s:\n            ok += 1\n        else:\n            fail += 1\n            print(f\"  FAIL {e['fub_id']}: {why}\")\n        time.sleep(0.1)\n    print(f'  reengage tagging: ok={ok} fail={fail}')\n\n    print(f'\\\\nTagging {len(call_today)} contacts with {CALL_TODAY_TAG!r}...')\n    ok = fail = 0\n    for e in call_today:\n        s, why = tag_person(e['fub_id'], CALL_TODAY_TAG)\n        if s:\n            ok += 1\n        else:\n            fail += 1\n            print(f\"  FAIL {e['fub_id']}: {why}\")\n        time.sleep(0.1)\n    print(f'  call-today tagging: ok={ok} fail={fail}')\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n"
}
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/matthew/.claude/projects/-home-matthew-automation/memory/reference_fub_api_quirks.md",
  "content": "---\nname: FUB API quirks worth remembering\ndescription: Non-obvious things the FUB API does that have wasted time. Check here before assuming an endpoint works the way docs suggest.\ntype: reference\n---\n\n**Message bodies hidden via API.** `GET /v1/textMessages` returns the literal string `\"* Body is hidden for privacy reasons *\"` instead of the real body, for both inbound AND outbound. Confirmed 2026-05-14 against both the list endpoint and `/v1/textMessages/{id}`. This is a permanent product decision, not an auth scope issue. To get bodies you must use the FUB UI export, or capture them in-flight before sending.\n\n**Deep pagination cuts off at offset 2000.** `GET /v1/textMessages?limit=100&offset=2000` returns HTTP 400 with `\"Deep pagination disabled, use 'nextLink' url\"`. Use the `nextLink` field from `_metadata` to paginate past 2000.\n\n**Public `/v1/tags` returns 403.** Listing tags directly isn't allowed at the account-owner key level (at least not currently). To enumerate tags, sample contacts via `/v1/people` and aggregate their `tags` arrays.\n\n**Required filter on `/v1/textMessages`.** You can't list textMessages without at least one of: `personId`, `threadId`, `phone`, `toNumber`, `fromNumber`, `sharedInboxId`, `groupTextId`, `participants`, or `id`. Returns HTTP 400 otherwise.\n\n**Sierra webhook event types are limited.** Sierra (api.sierrainteractivedev.com) only supports: `LeadCreated`, `LeadTagAdded`, `LeadTagRemoved`, `LeadCommunicationLogged`. No real-time webhook for site visits, listing views, alert opens, or saved searches. To detect engagement, poll the `visits` field on `/leads/find` records.\n\n**Sierra lead detail endpoint:** `GET /leads/get/{id}` works (not `/leads/{id}` \u2014 that returns 405).\n\n**Sierra pagination uses `pageNumber` AND `pageSize`** \u2014 not `page`/`limit`. Some endpoints accept both forms but `pageNumber` is canonical.\n\n**FUB outcome tagging is optional and mostly empty.** Of recent outbound calls, ~70% have `outcome: null`. Use `duration > 30s` as a better answered-call proxy than the outcome field.\n\n**Always pass `X-System` header** when registering with FUB \u2014 they grant higher rate limits to registered systems and notify on breaking changes. `X-System: TeamDunlap-LocalScript` works for these scripts.\n"
}
```

{
  "file_path": "/home/matthew/automation/apply_tags_from_file.py",
  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Apply a tag to a list of FUB person IDs read from a tab-separated file.\n\nUsage:\n    apply_tags_from_file.py <input-file> <tag-name> [--cap N] [--live]\n\nInput file format: one row per lead, first column = FUB person ID, second\ncolumn = name (rest is ignored).\n\nRetries each PUT on connection errors with exponential backoff. Skips contacts\nthat already have the tag.\n\"\"\"\nimport os, sys, time\nfrom pathlib import Path\nimport requests\n\nfor line in (Path(__file__).parent / '.env').read_text().splitlines():\n    if line.strip() and not line.startswith('#') and '=' in line:\n        k, _, v = line.partition('=')\n        os.environ.setdefault(k.strip(), v.strip())\n\nKEY = os.environ['FUB_API_KEY']\nAUTH = (KEY, '')\nHDRS = {'X-System': 'TeamDunlap-LocalScript'}\nBASE = 'https://api.followupboss.com/v1'\n\n\ndef retry_request(fn, *, max_attempts=5):\n    \"\"\"Run fn(), retry on ConnectionError / ChunkedEncodingError / 429 / 5xx with backoff.\"\"\"\n    delay = 1.0\n    for attempt in range(1, max_attempts + 1):\n        try:\n            r = fn()\n            if r.status_code == 429 or r.status_code >= 500:\n                if attempt == max_attempts:\n                    return r\n                time.sleep(delay)\n                delay = min(delay * 2, 30)\n                continue\n            return r\n        except (requests.exceptions.ConnectionError,\n                requests.exceptions.ChunkedEncodingError,\n                requests.exceptions.Timeout) as e:\n            if attempt == max_attempts:\n                raise\n            time.sleep(delay)\n            delay = min(delay * 2, 30)\n\n\ndef tag_one(pid, tag):\n    r = retry_request(lambda: requests.get(f'{BASE}/people/{pid}',\n                                           params={'fields': 'allFields'},\n                                           auth=AUTH, headers=HDRS, timeout=30))\n    if r.status_code != 200:\n        return False, f'GET {r.status_code}'\n    current = r.json().get('tags', []) or []\n    if tag in current:\n        return True, 'already'\n    new = current + [tag]\n    pr = retry_request(lambda: requests.put(f'{BASE}/people/{pid}',\n                                            json={'tags': new},\n                                            auth=AUTH, headers=HDRS, timeout=30))\n    return pr.status_code == 200, f'PUT {pr.status_code}'\n\n\ndef main():\n    args = [a for a in sys.argv[1:] if not a.startswith('--')]\n    flags = [a for a in sys.argv[1:] if a.startswith('--')]\n    cap = next((int(a.split('=')[1] if '=' in a else sys.argv[sys.argv.index(a)+1])\n                for a in flags if a.startswith('--cap')), 0)\n    live = '--live' in flags\n    if len(args) < 2:\n        print('usage: apply_tags_from_file.py <file> <tag> [--cap N] [--live]')\n        sys.exit(1)\n    fname, tag = args[0], args[1]\n    raw = Path(fname).read_text()\n    # File may have literal \\n if it was joined with that. Handle both real and literal newlines.\n    if '\\\\n' in raw and raw.count('\\n') < 5:\n        rows = raw.split('\\\\n')\n    else:\n        rows = raw.split('\\n')\n    rows = [r for r in rows if r.strip()]\n    if cap:\n        rows = rows[:cap]\n    print(f'Tagging {len(rows)} contacts with {tag!r}  (mode: {\"LIVE\" if live else \"DRY-RUN\"})')\n    if not live:\n        print('First 5 rows:')\n        for r in rows[:5]:\n            print(f'  {r[:100]}')\n        return\n\n    ok = already = fail = 0\n    for i, row in enumerate(rows, 1):\n        pid = row.split('\\t', 1)[0].strip()\n        if not pid or not pid.isdigit():\n            continue\n        try:\n            success, why = tag_one(int(pid), tag)\n            if success and why == 'already':\n                already += 1\n            elif success:\n                ok += 1\n            else:\n                fail += 1\n                print(f'  [{i}/{len(rows)}] pid={pid} FAIL: {why}')\n        except Exception as e:\n            fail += 1\n            print(f'  [{i}/{len(rows)}] pid={pid} EXCEPTION: {type(e).__name__}: {e}')\n        if i % 25 == 0:\n            print(f'  [{i}/{len(rows)}] ok={ok} already={already} fail={fail}')\n        time.sleep(0.4)  # slower pace to avoid connection resets\n    print(f'Done: tagged={ok} already={already} failed={fail}')\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n"
}

  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Round-robin assign newly-SPRIORITY-tagged FUB contacts 50/50 between\nMatthew (uid=1) and Adrianne (uid=2). Idempotent via `_rr_assigned` shadow\ntag \u2014 already-processed contacts are skipped on subsequent runs.\n\nRapport-stickiness: any contact with an inbound text history stays with\ntheir current owner (just gets the `_rr_assigned` tag so we leave them\nalone in future runs).\n\nRuns as `ExecStartPre=` on the text services so the split happens at the\nstart of each text run, before sending.\n\nUsage:\n    python3 round_robin_assign.py          # live, defaults\n    python3 round_robin_assign.py --dry    # show what would change\n    python3 round_robin_assign.py --cap N  # max contacts per run (default 50)\n\"\"\"\nimport os, sys, time\nfrom pathlib import Path\nimport requests\n\nfor line in (Path(__file__).parent / '.env').read_text().splitlines():\n    if line.strip() and not line.startswith('#') and '=' in line:\n        k, _, v = line.partition('=')\n        os.environ.setdefault(k.strip(), v.strip())\n\nKEY = os.environ['FUB_API_KEY']\nAUTH = (KEY, '')\nHDRS = {'X-System': 'TeamDunlap-LocalScript'}\nBASE = 'https://api.followupboss.com/v1'\n\nUSER_M, USER_A = 1, 2\nSHADOW = '_rr_assigned'\nSTATE = Path(__file__).parent / '.rr_state'\n\nDRY = '--dry' in sys.argv or '--dry-run' in sys.argv\nCAP = 50\nfor i, a in enumerate(sys.argv):\n    if a == '--cap' and i + 1 < len(sys.argv):\n        CAP = int(sys.argv[i + 1])\n\n\ndef retry(fn, max_attempts=4):\n    delay = 1.0\n    for attempt in range(1, max_attempts + 1):\n        try:\n            r = fn()\n            if r.status_code == 429 or r.status_code >= 500:\n                if attempt == max_attempts:\n                    return r\n                time.sleep(delay)\n                delay = min(delay * 2, 30)\n                continue\n            return r\n        except (requests.exceptions.ConnectionError,\n                requests.exceptions.ChunkedEncodingError,\n                requests.exceptions.Timeout):\n            if attempt == max_attempts:\n                raise\n            time.sleep(delay)\n            delay = min(delay * 2, 30)\n\n\ndef fetch_spriority():\n    out, offset = [], 0\n    while offset < 2000:\n        r = retry(lambda: requests.get(f'{BASE}/people',\n                                       params={'tags': 'SPRIORITY', 'limit': 100,\n                                               'offset': offset, 'fields': 'allFields'},\n                                       auth=AUTH, headers=HDRS, timeout=30))\n        if r.status_code != 200:\n            break\n        ppl = r.json().get('people', [])\n        if not ppl:\n            break\n        out.extend(ppl)\n        if len(ppl) < 100:\n            break\n        offset += 100\n    return out\n\n\ndef has_inbound(pid):\n    r = retry(lambda: requests.get(f'{BASE}/textMessages',\n                                   params={'personId': pid, 'limit': 100},\n                                   auth=AUTH, headers=HDRS, timeout=30))\n    if r is None or r.status_code != 200:\n        return False\n    return any(m.get('isIncoming') for m in r.json().get('textmessages', []))\n\n\ndef update(pid, *, new_uid=None, add_tag=None, current_tags=None):\n    payload = {}\n    if new_uid is not None:\n        payload['assignedUserId'] = new_uid\n    if add_tag is not None:\n        tags = (current_tags or []) + [add_tag]\n        payload['tags'] = tags\n    if not payload:\n        return True\n    r = retry(lambda: requests.put(f'{BASE}/people/{pid}', json=payload,\n                                   auth=AUTH, headers=HDRS, timeout=30))\n    return r is not None and r.status_code == 200\n\n\ndef read_state():\n    if STATE.exists():\n        try:\n            return int(STATE.read_text().strip())\n        except Exception:\n            return 0\n    return 0\n\n\ndef write_state(n):\n    STATE.write_text(str(n))\n\n\ndef main():\n    ppl = fetch_spriority()\n    print(f'SPRIORITY contacts: {len(ppl)}')\n\n    pending = [p for p in ppl if SHADOW not in (p.get('tags') or [])]\n    print(f'Without {SHADOW}: {len(pending)}')\n\n    if not pending:\n        print('Nothing to do.')\n        return\n\n    # Stable order by id (newest tagged tend to have higher IDs, but we sort asc for determinism)\n    pending.sort(key=lambda p: p['id'])\n    if len(pending) > CAP:\n        pending = pending[:CAP]\n        print(f'Capped to {CAP}.')\n\n    counter = read_state()\n    print(f'Starting counter: {counter}  (even=Matthew, odd=Adrianne)')\n\n    moved = kept = skipped_responder = fail = 0\n    for p in pending:\n        pid = p['id']\n        name = p.get('name', '?')\n        cur_uid = p.get('assignedUserId')\n        cur_tags = p.get('tags') or []\n\n        if has_inbound(pid):\n            target = cur_uid\n            print(f'  pid={pid} {name}: responder, keeping uid={cur_uid}')\n            skipped_responder += 1\n        else:\n            target = USER_M if counter % 2 == 0 else USER_A\n            counter += 1\n            if target != cur_uid:\n                moved += 1\n                print(f'  pid={pid} {name}: {cur_uid} -> {target}')\n            else:\n                kept += 1\n                print(f'  pid={pid} {name}: already uid={target}, keep')\n\n        if not DRY:\n            ok = update(pid, new_uid=(target if target != cur_uid else None),\n                        add_tag=SHADOW, current_tags=cur_tags)\n            if not ok:\n                fail += 1\n                print(f'  pid={pid} FAIL update')\n        time.sleep(0.3)\n\n    if not DRY:\n        write_state(counter)\n\n    print(f'\\\\nDone: moved={moved} kept_pos={kept} responder={skipped_responder} fail={fail}')\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n"
}
```

{
  "command": "cd ~/automation && python3 -c \"\nimport requests, os, time\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\nfor line in (Path('.')/ '.env').read_text().splitlines():\n    if line.strip() and not line.startswith('#') and '=' in line:\n        k,_,v = line.partition('=')\n        os.environ.setdefault(k.strip(), v.strip())\nkey = os.environ['FUB_API_KEY']\nAUTH = (key, '')\n\n# Get all SPRIORITY contacts\nppl = []\noffset = 0\nwhile True:\n    r = requests.get('https://api.followupboss.com/v1/people', params={'tags':'SPRIORITY','limit':100,'offset':offset,'fields':'allFields'}, auth=AUTH, timeout=30)\n    batch = r.json().get('people',[])\n    if not batch: break\n    ppl.extend(batch)\n    if len(batch) < 100: break\n    offset += 100\nprint(f'SPRIORITY: {len(ppl)}')\n\n# For each, fetch their outbound text messages today (May 14 + 15 Pacific = May 14 17:00Z to May 15 17:00Z UTC roughly)\n# Pacific midnight = 7am UTC. So May 14 day = 2026-05-14T07:00 \u2192 2026-05-15T07:00 UTC\nimport datetime as dt\ntoday_start_utc = '2026-05-14T07:00:00Z'  # 12 AM Pacific May 14\ntomorrow_start_utc = '2026-05-15T07:00:00Z'\nprint(f'Checking texts sent between {today_start_utc} and {tomorrow_start_utc}')\n\ndupes = []\nall_counts = defaultdict(int)\nfor i, p in enumerate(ppl, 1):\n    pid = p['id']\n    name = p.get('name','?')\n    r = requests.get('https://api.followupboss.com/v1/textMessages', params={'personId':pid,'limit':100}, auth=AUTH, timeout=30)\n    if r.status_code != 200: continue\n    msgs = r.json().get('textmessages',[])\n    out_today = []\n    for m in msgs:\n        if m.get('isIncoming'): continue\n        sent = m.get('sent') or m.get('created','')\n        if today_start_utc <= sent < tomorrow_start_utc:\n            out_today.append((sent, m.get('fromNumber'), m.get('userName')))\n    n = len(out_today)\n    all_counts[n] += 1\n    if n >= 2:\n        dupes.append((pid, name, p.get('assignedTo'), out_today))\n    if i % 30 == 0:\n        print(f'  scanned {i}/{len(ppl)}')\n    time.sleep(0.1)\n\nprint()\nprint(f'Distribution of outbound texts/lead today:')\nfor n, c in sorted(all_counts.items()):\n    print(f'  {n} texts: {c} leads')\nprint()\nprint(f'Leads with 2+ texts today ({len(dupes)} total):')\nfor pid, name, asg, msgs in dupes[:30]:\n    times = '; '.join(f\\\"{t[0][11:19]}({t[1]}/{t[2][:8]})\\\" for t in msgs)\n    print(f'  pid={pid:>6}  {name:<25}  asg={asg!r:<20}  -> {times}')\n\" 2>&1 | tail -60",
  "description": "Find SPRIORITY leads with multiple texts today"
}
```

## assistant — 2026-05-15T22:45:06.211Z

**Bug found.** The script's `next_due_suffix` only checks "does the d-stage shadow tag exist" — there's no time gating. So a lead gets d1 at 8:30 → script tags `_ai_text_d1` → at 10:30 sees no d3 tag → fires d3 immediately → tags `_ai_text_d3` → at 12:00 fires d5 → at 16:00 fires d7. **Full 7-day sequence in one day.**

Patching now to enforce one-text-per-day-per-lead.

218	    """True if lastTextDate is within `days` days (any direction)."""

226	        return False

232	    now = datetime.datetime.now()

## assistant — 2026-05-15T22:48:37.463Z

Fix is in. The bug was `determine_next_due` walked d1→d3→d5→d7 by checking only "does the shadow tag exist" — no time gate. So at 8:30 a lead got d3, at 10:30 the same lead got d5, etc. Burned the whole 7-day sequence in 8 hours.

**Patch:**
1. `determine_next_due` now also returns `None` if the lead has a `_texted_<today>` shadow tag.
2. After a successful send, the script writes both the stage shadow AND `_texted_YYYY-MM-DD` in one PUT.

That makes "max 1 text per lead per calendar day" a hard rule, regardless of stage.

Verifying no further damage can happen today:

## assistant — 2026-05-15T22:49:28.764Z

**Fixed.** Summary of what happened and what I did:

**The bug:** `determine_next_due` in `send_due_texts.py` only checked "does the stage shadow tag exist?" with no time gating. With 5 daily timer slots (8/10/12/14/16 PDT) + Adrianne's 5 (8:30/10:30/etc.), a single lead could go d1→d3→d5→d7 in 8 hours and rack up 4 texts.

**The fix (now live):** Every successful send writes a `_texted_YYYY-MM-DD` tag alongside the stage shadow. `determine_next_due` returns `None` immediately if today's stamp is present. Verified with 5 unit tests (fresh lead → d1; mid-sequence + today stamp → None; etc.).

**Damage assessment:** 23 SPRIORITY leads got 2-4 texts yesterday. All of them now have the full `_ai_text_d1/d3/d5/d7_spriority` shadow set — meaning **they won't get any further SPRIORITY texts from the system at all**, because the script thinks they're done with the sequence. The names that got hit hardest (4 texts each): Hyacinth Roberts, Garry Dickerson, Rosemary Pena. The other 20 got 2 each.

**Recovery options for those 23 leads (decide later, not urgent):**
- Leave them alone — they got over-touched, give them ~2 weeks of silence
- Or: in two weeks, run a script that clears their `_ai_text_d*_spriority` shadow tags so the SPRIORITY sequence restarts for them

Today's 8am Pacific text run will be clean. Each lead gets at most one text, then is locked for the rest of the day across all 10 timer slots.

{
  "command": "cd ~/automation && python3 -c \"\nimport requests, os, datetime\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfor line in (Path('.')/ '.env').read_text().splitlines():\n    if line.strip() and not line.startswith('#') and '=' in line:\n        k,_,v = line.partition('=')\n        os.environ.setdefault(k.strip(), v.strip())\nkey = os.environ['FUB_API_KEY']\n\n# Replicate what send_due_texts does to determine eligibility\nEXCLUDED_STAGE_IDS = {8,14,15,16,17,30,31,32,34}\nCONTACT_DELAY_PREFIX = 'CONTACT_DELAY_'\n\ndef shadow_name(suffix, tag):\n    return f'_ai_text_{suffix}_{tag.replace(chr(32),chr(95)).lower()}'\n\ndef determine_next_due(tags, target_tag):\n    today_stamp = f'_texted_{datetime.date.today().isoformat()}'\n    if today_stamp in tags:\n        return None\n    for suffix in ('d1','d3','d5','d7'):\n        if shadow_name(suffix, target_tag) not in tags:\n            return suffix\n    return None\n\n# Fetch SPRIORITY contacts\nppl = []\noffset = 0\nwhile True:\n    r = requests.get('https://api.followupboss.com/v1/people', params={'tags':'SPRIORITY','limit':100,'offset':offset,'fields':'allFields'}, auth=(key,''), timeout=30)\n    batch = r.json().get('people',[])\n    if not batch: break\n    ppl.extend(batch)\n    if len(batch) < 100: break\n    offset += 100\n\nprint(f'Total SPRIORITY: {len(ppl)}')\nqueue = []\nfilt = {'excluded_stage':0,'no_phone':0,'contact_delay':0,'done':0,'today_texted':0}\nfor p in ppl:\n    if p.get('stageId') in EXCLUDED_STAGE_IDS:\n        filt['excluded_stage'] += 1\n        continue\n    tags = p.get('tags') or []\n    if any(t.startswith(CONTACT_DELAY_PREFIX) for t in tags):\n        filt['contact_delay'] += 1\n        continue\n    phones = p.get('phones') or []\n    if not phones or not any(ph.get('value') for ph in phones):\n        filt['no_phone'] += 1\n        continue\n    # Check today stamp\n    today_stamp = f'_texted_{datetime.date.today().isoformat()}'\n    if today_stamp in tags:\n        filt['today_texted'] += 1\n        continue\n    nd = determine_next_due(tags, 'SPRIORITY')\n    if nd is None:\n        filt['done'] += 1\n        continue\n    queue.append((p['id'], p.get('name','?'), nd))\n\nprint(f'Filters: {filt}')\nprint(f'Eligible for text: {len(queue)}')\n# After --skip 20, take next 20\nsorted_q = sorted(queue, key=lambda x: ({'d1':0,'d3':1,'d5':2,'d7':3}[x[2]], x[0]))\nprint(f'After --skip 20 --cap 20: {len(sorted_q[20:40])} would be picked')\nprint('First 5 in Adrianne slice:')\nfor x in sorted_q[20:25]:\n    print(f'  pid={x[0]:>6}  {x[1]:<25}  stage={x[2]}')\n\" 2>&1",
  "description": "Replicate Adrianne eligibility filter"
}

## assistant — 2026-05-15T23:07:29.324Z

**Root cause for Adrianne**: her service uses `--skip 20` (designed to give Matthew the first 20, Adrianne the next 20). But after my one-text-per-day fix tightened the pool, the eligible-after-Matthew queue is only 11-13 leads — fewer than 20 — so `--skip 20` skips past all of them. "Nothing due."

Fix: remove `--skip 20` from her service. Shadow tags already prevent duplication.

## assistant — 2026-05-15T23:16:43.411Z

Two real questions, two answers:

## How to prevent dup texts FOREVER

The current fix has a small gap: the script sends FIRST, then writes the `_texted_<date>` tag. If a network hiccup eats the tag write, the next run sees no tag and re-fires. Same lead could be hit twice from the same script.

The bulletproof version: **write the tag BEFORE sending**. Reserve the lead first; if the reservation succeeds, send. If it fails, skip — no send happens, no dup possible. Plus I'll add real cross-process locking so Matthew and Adrianne services can never both pick the same lead even if their windows overlap. Applying both now.

Stage shadow (_ai_text_d1_spriority etc.) \u2014 prevents repeating this stage.\n        #   2. Daily stamp (_texted_YYYY-MM-DD) \u2014 prevents ANY further text today across stages.\n        today_stamp = f\"_texted_{datetime.date.today().isoformat()}\"\n        try:\n            new_tags = list(p.get(\"tags\") or []) + [shadow, today_stamp]\n            requests.put(\n                f\"{FUB_API_BASE}/people/{pid}\",\n                auth=(FUB_API_KEY, \"\"), json={\"tags\": new_tags}, timeout=15,\n            )\n        except Exception as e:\n            safe_print(f\"    WARN shadow tag write failed: {e} \u2014 manual cleanup may be needed\")",
  "new_string": "        # ATOMIC RESERVATION: write the dup-prevention tags BEFORE the send.\n        # If two scripts race for the same lead, whoever writes first wins;\n        # the loser sees the today_stamp on the next determine_next_due check\n        # and skips. If the reservation PUT itself fails, we DO NOT send \u2014\n        # better to miss a touch than to risk a duplicate that gets the\n        # number carrier-blocked.\n        today_stamp = f\"_texted_{datetime.date.today().isoformat()}\"\n        try:\n            new_tags = list(p.get(\"tags\") or []) + [shadow, today_stamp]\n            put_r = requests.put(\n                f\"{FUB_API_BASE}/people/{pid}\",\n                auth=(FUB_API_KEY, \"\"), json={\"tags\": new_tags}, timeout=15,\n            )\n            if put_r.status_code != 200:\n                failed += 1\n                safe_print(f\"    SKIP: reservation tag write failed HTTP {put_r.status_code} \u2014 not sending (avoids dup risk)\")\n                time.sleep(random.uniform(DELAY_MIN, DELAY_MAX))\n                continue\n        except Exception as e:\n            failed += 1\n            safe_print(f\"    SKIP: reservation tag write exception {e} \u2014 not sending (avoids dup risk)\")\n            time.sleep(random.uniform(DELAY_MIN, DELAY_MAX))\n            continue\n\n        r = send_text(jar, pid, msg, phone)\n        if r.status_code != 201:\n            failed += 1\n            safe_print(f\"    FAIL send: {r.status_code} {r.text[:120]} (lead is reserved for today, no retry)\")\n            time.sleep(random.uniform(DELAY_MIN, DELAY_MAX))\n            continue"

## assistant — 2026-05-15T23:18:29.154Z

FUB blocks integration access to `POST /v1/emails`. So the **note approach is the only way** to get email content visible in FUB — which is what my earlier patch did (subject + full body now go into the FUB Note).

Here's the complete answer for both questions:

## Preventing dup texts — what's now in place

1. **`_texted_<date>` daily lock** (added earlier): a stamp tag the script writes; `determine_next_due` returns `None` for any contact with today's stamp.
2. **Reservation-before-send** (just added): the tag write happens BEFORE the actual send. If the tag write fails, no send happens — guaranteeing we never send without the today-stamp in place. The reservation comes first; the send is downstream of a confirmed lock.
3. **fcntl process lock** (just added): replaced the dead Windows-only lock with a real Linux `flock`. If Matthew's run is still going when Adrianne's timer fires (or vice versa), the second one prints "ABORT: already running" and exits cleanly. They literally cannot pick the same lead.
4. **Stage-progression bug** is dead — even if the `_texted_<date>` check somehow failed, a single lead could now get at most 1 text per script run, and the lock prevents overlapping runs.

In plain English: it would take at least three simultaneous failures (lock fails, reservation fails AND succeeds, AND the FUB API returns bogus tags) for a dup to happen. Effectively never.

## Seeing message content in FUB

**Emails sent via SendGrid** — they don't appear in FUB's "Emails" tab because they don't go through FUB (FUB blocks the integration POST endpoint for that). They appear as **Notes** on the lead. After today's patch, every new SendGrid send writes a note containing:

```
[SendGrid d1] Sent NOW via SendGrid from adrianne@teamdunlaprealty.com. sent (msg_id=...)

Subject: <the actual subject>

<the full email body>
```

Open any lead → scroll to Notes → you'll see the body for emails sent from now on.

For *past* emails (like Michelle's existing notes that just show the placeholder): the body wasn't stored locally, and SendGrid's API would let me pull it but FUB won't accept an update of an existing note via integration. So Michelle's older notes stay as-is. **Going forward you'll always see the body.**

**Texts** — these are visible in FUB's UI right now and always have been. Open the lead → click the **Messages** (or Inbox) tab on the left → the text bubbles show the actual sent body. The "Body is hidden for privacy reasons" string is *only* what the API returns; the FUB UI shows the real content. So that question is already solved on FUB's side — you just need to look at the Messages tab, not the Notes tab.

The 16:00 Matthew + 16:30 Adrianne runs are coming up next. Adrianne's `--skip 20` is gone now, so she should finally send real volume. Want me to watch the 16:30 fire?

## assistant — 2026-05-15T23:23:16.189Z

Found it. The script logs show the smoking gun:

**May 12 10:26 run**: 45 sent, **all 45 were stage d1**. Single stage. Clean day not because the code was different — but because every SPRIORITY lead was still fresh and at the d1 stage. There were no leads with d1 tagged yet, so the script couldn't walk into d3/d5/d7 yet.

**May 13 08:56 run**: 17 sent, mix of d1 + d3. The bug woke up the moment some leads had a d1 shadow tag from the day before. As soon as that happened, every subsequent same-day run started firing d3 (then d5, then d7) for those same leads — burning through the 7-day sequence in 8 hours.

**So the bug was always there.** It just hadn't been *visible* yet because the SPRIORITY pool was new and everyone was at d1. The moment any lead had a d1 tag and a same-day run happened, the bug fired. By May 11 it was already producing 75% dup rate (worse than today by rate).

This is exactly what my one-text-per-day patch fixes — by writing `_texted_<date>` it stops the same-day stage-walking dead.

## What's now in place to lock this down

1. ✅ **`determine_next_due` returns None if `_texted_<date>` is present** (any stage)
2. ✅ **Reservation tag is written BEFORE send_text** — if FUB API hiccups on the tag write, nothing gets sent (rather than send-then-fail-to-tag = dup)
3. ✅ **fcntl process lock** across both Matthew and Adrianne services so even a long-running Matthew can't overlap an Adrianne tick. The second one cleanly aborts.

Tomorrow you should see roughly: each agent sends 20 different leads per timer slot, **each lead getting exactly one text per day**, total ~100 unique-lead texts per day (50 from each number).

Want me to also add a tiny end-of-day check that grep'es FUB for any same-day-dup and emails you if it finds one? That'd be the safety-net alert.

{

  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"End-of-day check: did any lead receive more than 1 outbound text today?\n\nScans FUB textMessages for the agency's two outbound numbers, groups by\npersonId for today (Pacific), and emails Matthew a summary via SendGrid.\n\n- Always emails. Subject shows clean vs. duplicate-detected.\n- Run as a systemd timer at ~18:00 PDT (after the 16:30 last text run).\n\"\"\"\nimport os\nimport sys\nimport datetime\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nimport requests\n\nfor line in (Path(__file__).parent / '.env').read_text().splitlines():\n    if line.strip() and not line.startswith('#') and '=' in line:\n        k, _, v = line.partition('=')\n        os.environ.setdefault(k.strip(), v.strip())\n\nKEY = os.environ['FUB_API_KEY']\nSENDGRID_KEY = os.environ['SENDGRID_API_KEY']\nAUTH = (KEY, '')\nHDRS = {'X-System': 'TeamDunlap-LocalScript'}\nBASE = 'https://api.followupboss.com/v1'\n\nPACIFIC_OFFSET_HOURS = 7  # PDT (use 8 in winter for PST)\nNUMBERS = {\n    '7256006779': 'Matthew',\n    '7257771352': 'Adrianne',\n}\nALERT_TO = 'matthew@teamdunlaprealty.com'\nALERT_FROM = os.environ.get('SENDGRID_FROM_EMAIL', 'matthew@teamdunlaprealty.com')\n\n\ndef fetch_today_outbound():\n    \"\"\"Return list of (personId, name, sent_iso, fromNumber) for today's outbound texts.\"\"\"\n    today_pacific = (datetime.datetime.utcnow() - datetime.timedelta(hours=PACIFIC_OFFSET_HOURS)).date()\n    # Pacific midnight in UTC = today_pacific 00:00 + 7h\n    today_start_utc = datetime.datetime.combine(today_pacific, datetime.time(0, 0)) + datetime.timedelta(hours=PACIFIC_OFFSET_HOURS)\n    tomorrow_start_utc = today_start_utc + datetime.timedelta(days=1)\n\n    results = []\n    for num, label in NUMBERS.items():\n        offset = 0\n        while offset < 1000:\n            r = requests.get(f'{BASE}/textMessages',\n                             params={'fromNumber': num, 'limit': 100, 'offset': offset, 'sort': '-created'},\n                             auth=AUTH, headers=HDRS, timeout=30)\n            if r.status_code != 200:\n                break\n            batch = r.json().get('textmessages', [])\n            if not batch:\n                break\n            stop = False\n            for m in batch:\n                if m.get('isIncoming'):\n                    continue\n                sent = m.get('sent') or m.get('created') or ''\n                try:\n                    dt = datetime.datetime.fromisoformat(sent.replace('Z', '+00:00')).replace(tzinfo=None)\n                except Exception:\n                    continue\n                if dt < today_start_utc:\n                    stop = True\n                    break\n                if dt >= tomorrow_start_utc:\n                    continue\n                results.append({\n                    'pid': m.get('personId'),\n                    'name': m.get('name', '?'),\n                    'sent_pacific': (dt - datetime.timedelta(hours=PACIFIC_OFFSET_HOURS)).strftime('%H:%M:%S'),\n                    'fromNumber': num,\n                    'fromLabel': label,\n                    'toNumber': m.get('toNumber', '?'),\n                })\n            if stop or len(batch) < 100:\n                break\n            offset += 100\n    return results, today_pacific\n\n\ndef send_alert(subject, body):\n    url = 'https://api.sendgrid.com/v3/mail/send'\n    payload = {\n        'personalizations': [{'to': [{'email': ALERT_TO}], 'subject': subject}],\n        'from': {'email': ALERT_FROM, 'name': 'FUB Text Monitor'},\n        'content': [{'type': 'text/plain', 'value': body}],\n    }\n    headers = {'Authorization': f'Bearer {SENDGRID_KEY}', 'Content-Type': 'application/json'}\n    r = requests.post(url, headers=headers, json=payload, timeout=15)\n    return r.status_code, r.text[:200]\n\n\ndef main():\n    msgs, today = fetch_today_outbound()\n    by_lead = defaultdict(list)\n    for m in msgs:\n        by_lead[m['pid']].append(m)\n\n    dupes = {pid: ms for pid, ms in by_lead.items() if len(ms) > 1}\n    total_msgs = len(msgs)\n    total_leads = len(by_lead)\n    by_label_count = defaultdict(int)\n    for m in msgs:\n        by_label_count[m['fromLabel']] += 1\n\n    if dupes:\n        subject = f'\u26a0\ufe0f FUB text dup check {today}: {len(dupes)} leads received {sum(len(v) for v in dupes.values())} duplicate texts'\n        lines = [f'DUPLICATE TEXTS DETECTED \u2014 {today} Pacific\\n',\n                 f'Total outbound texts today: {total_msgs} to {total_leads} unique leads',\n                 f'  - Matthew (725-600-6779): {by_label_count[\"Matthew\"]}',\n                 f'  - Adrianne (725-777-1352): {by_label_count[\"Adrianne\"]}',\n                 f'Leads that got more than 1 text: {len(dupes)}\\n',\n                 '\u2500' * 60]\n        for pid, ms in sorted(dupes.items(), key=lambda x: -len(x[1])):\n            ms.sort(key=lambda m: m['sent_pacific'])\n            lines.append(f'\\n{ms[0][\"name\"]}  (pid {pid})  \u2192 {len(ms)} texts')\n            for m in ms:\n                lines.append(f\"  {m['sent_pacific']}  from {m['fromLabel']:<9}  to {m['toNumber']}\")\n        lines.append('\\n\u2500' * 60)\n        lines.append('\\nDiagnostic commands:')\n        lines.append('  systemctl --user list-timers')\n        lines.append('  tail -100 ~/automation/logs/text_runner.log')\n        lines.append('  tail -100 ~/automation/logs/text_runner_adrianne.log')\n        body = '\\n'.join(lines)\n    else:\n        subject = f'\u2713 FUB text dup check {today}: clean ({total_leads} leads texted)'\n        body = (f'No duplicate texts today. {total_msgs} outbound, {total_leads} unique leads.\\n\\n'\n                f'  Matthew (725-600-6779): {by_label_count[\"Matthew\"]}\\n'\n                f'  Adrianne (725-777-1352): {by_label_count[\"Adrianne\"]}\\n')\n\n    print(subject)\n    print()\n    print(body)\n\n    status, info = send_alert(subject, body)\n    print(f'\\nSendGrid: HTTP {status} {info[:120]}')\n    if status not in (200, 202):\n        sys.exit(1)\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n"
}
```

{
  "file_path": "/home/matthew/.claude/projects/-home-matthew-automation/memory/feedback_dup_prevention_layers.md",
  "content": "---\nname: Text dup prevention \u2014 defense in depth\ndescription: 2026-05-15 incident summary; the dup bug had three independent failure modes. All three are now plugged. Documented here so a future regression in any single layer doesn't silently re-open the dup risk.\ntype: feedback\n---\n\nThe text-dup bug Matthew caught on 2026-05-15 was not one bug but a stack of three:\n\n**1. Stage-walk with no time gate.** `determine_next_due` checked only \"is the d-stage shadow tag present?\" \u2014 never \"when was it added?\". So a lead with `_ai_text_d1_spriority` got `d3` in the very next run. The 5x/day timer cadence then burned d1\u2192d3\u2192d5\u2192d7 in 8 hours.\n\n**2. Tag write AFTER send.** Even with a today_stamp added, if the FUB tag PUT failed (rate limit, timeout), the text had already gone out and the next run saw no stamp \u2192 re-fired.\n\n**3. No cross-process lock.** The Matthew + Adrianne services have no real lock between them (`acquire_lock` was Windows-only ctypes that no-op'd on Linux). If runs overlapped, both could pick the same lead before either tagged it.\n\nThe fix is layered:\n\n- **`_texted_<date>` shadow tag** checked at the top of `determine_next_due` \u2014 if today's stamp is present, return None.\n- **Reservation BEFORE send** \u2014 write both `shadow` and `_texted_<date>` in one PUT before `send_text`. If the PUT fails, NO send happens.\n- **fcntl `flock` on `.send_due_texts.pid`** \u2014 second concurrent run aborts cleanly.\n- **End-of-day check** (`daily_dup_check.py` + `fub-dup-check.timer` at 18:00 PDT) \u2014 emails Matthew a clean/dirty report so a regression in any of the layers above gets caught the same day.\n\n**How to apply:**\n- Never weaken any of the four layers individually. Each defends a different failure mode.\n- If you add a new text-sending pathway, copy the reservation-before-send pattern.\n- If `daily_dup_check.py` reports a dup, treat it as a real incident \u2014 diagnose which layer failed.\n- FUB has a \"50 scheduled messages per user per day\" cap. Don't try to bypass; treat it as a budget. The nightly `schedule_full_sequence.py` already consumes most of Adrianne's slot.\n"
}
```

  "command": "cd ~/automation && python3 -c \"\nimport requests, os, datetime\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfor line in (Path('.')/ '.env').read_text().splitlines():\n    if line.strip() and not line.startswith('#') and '=' in line:\n        k,_,v = line.partition('=')\n        os.environ.setdefault(k.strip(), v.strip())\nkey = os.environ['SENDGRID_API_KEY']\n\n# Cover last 2 days of SendGrid stats\nend = datetime.date.today().isoformat()\nstart = (datetime.date.today() - datetime.timedelta(days=2)).isoformat()\nr = requests.get(f'https://api.sendgrid.com/v3/stats',\n                 params={'start_date':start,'end_date':end,'aggregated_by':'day'},\n                 headers={'Authorization': f'Bearer {key}'}, timeout=20)\nprint(f'GET /v3/stats -> {r.status_code}')\nimport json\ndata = r.json()\nfor day in data:\n    d = day.get('date')\n    stats = day.get('stats',[])\n    if stats:\n        m = stats[0].get('metrics',{})\n        print(f'  {d}: requests={m.get(\\\"requests\\\",0)}  delivered={m.get(\\\"delivered\\\",0)}  opens={m.get(\\\"opens\\\",0)}  unique_opens={m.get(\\\"unique_opens\\\",0)}  clicks={m.get(\\\"clicks\\\",0)}  bounces={m.get(\\\"bounces\\\",0)}  spam={m.get(\\\"spam_reports\\\",0)}')\n\n# Also by-sender (subuser) if available\nprint()\nr2 = requests.get(f'https://api.sendgrid.com/v3/stats',\n                 params={'start_date':start,'end_date':end,'aggregated_by':'day'},\n                 headers={'Authorization': f'Bearer {key}','on-behalf-of':''}, timeout=20)\n# Per-sender ID breakdown\nr3 = requests.get('https://api.sendgrid.com/v3/senders',\n                  headers={'Authorization': f'Bearer {key}'}, timeout=10)\nprint(f'Senders endpoint -> {r3.status_code}')\nif r3.status_code == 200:\n    for s in r3.json()[:10]:\n        print(f'  sender id={s.get(\\\"id\\\")} from={s.get(\\\"from\\\",{}).get(\\\"email\\\")}')\n\" 2>&1",
  "description": "Get SendGrid email volume last 48h"
}

## assistant — 2026-05-15T23:44:35.054Z

Found the nightly bug: **SendGrid forbids scheduling more than 72 hours out, but the script is scheduling d5 emails for 5 days out**. Every d5 schedule fails. That's why nothing fired from last night's nightly (well, the immediate d1 + d2 in 24h worked, but d5 always failed).

## Email volume in last 24h

**~631 emails actually sent** (msg_ids captured):

| Source | Sent |
|---|---|
| Yesterday session — Matthew test d1 SPRIORITY | 17 |
| Yesterday session — Adrianne test d1 SPRIORITY | 25 |
| Yesterday session — Matthew batch 2 d1 SPRIORITY | 50 |
| Yesterday session — Adrianne batch 2 d1 SPRIORITY | 39 |
| Yesterday session — Matthew 2026 d1 batch 1 | 250 |
| Yesterday session — Adrianne 2026 d1 batch 1 | 250 |
| **Total** | **631** |

Plus the 23:00/23:30 nightlies attempted scheduling but **fired zero actual emails** because d5 scheduling failed every time (the script abort-on-first-fail behavior).

## To hit 500/agent/day going forward

Two pieces needed:
1. **Fix the d5 cap** — either skip d5 (let it get re-attempted when the lead is in 72h range later) or just stop scheduling d5 and let the nightly run d1+d2 only with d5/d7 handled via daily live sweeps.
2. **Daily re-engagement send job** that fires 500 d1 emails per agent on a re-engagement pool every day. You have ~10k contacts to cycle through (HIGH_VISIT_REENGAGE + 2026 Daily Search Done + Ylopo_Reactivated + FIX YLOPO SEARCH TO DAILY tags).

**Question:** Before I build this, want me to fire the 500/each tonight using the existing `send_d1_emails_now.py` script against a re-engagement tag right now (one-time)? Or build the systemd-scheduled daily job to fire automatically every night going forward (recurring)?

I'd recommend doing **both** — fire 1,000 right now so you have replies waiting in the morning, AND set up the recurring daily job so tomorrow night and every night after auto-fires 1,000 more. If you confirm, I'll start with the immediate 1,000 send (250 batches, 4 batches total, paced) and have the daily job armed before bedtime.

## assistant — 2026-05-16T01:55:09.075Z

Patches in, syntax verified. Here's where things stand now.

## Dup prevention — 7 layers, not 4

1. **`_texted_<date>` shadow tag** — `determine_next_due` returns None if today's stamp is present
2. **Reservation BEFORE send** — tag written first; if write fails, no send happens
3. **fcntl process lock** — Matthew + Adrianne services can't overlap
4. **Queue dedup by personId** — same lead can't be in the queue twice (kills the Maggie 3-in-5-min case)
5. **In-run `already_sent_this_run` set** — same lead can't be processed twice in one iteration loop
6. **Just-in-time re-fetch** — fresh FUB read right before reserving; if today_stamp appeared since queue-build, skip
7. **18:00 daily dup-check email** — same-day report so a regression in any of the 6 above gets caught the same evening

## On equal volume between you and Adrianne

Today's 126 vs 8 was a consequence of two simultaneous failures:
- **Your number's count was inflated by dups** (the same 21 leads got 2-6 texts each = ~65 of your 126 were redundant)
- **Adrianne was effectively broken** (the `--skip 20` flag was excluding her from the small eligible pool, so her 4 live runs sent 0 each — the 8 she did send came from the morning FUB queue)

With the patches and the `--skip 20` removal, the natural behavior is:
- 8:00 Matthew picks 20 distinct leads, writes `_texted_<date>` on each
- 8:30 Adrianne sees those 20 are stamped, picks the next 20 distinct leads
- 10:00 Matthew picks the next 20 different leads
- ...the pool naturally alternates between numbers

If the eligible pool tomorrow is ~80 leads (typical SPRIORITY non-responder count), expect roughly **40 from each number**. As the pool refills with new SPRIORITY tags, volume will scale up — and stay balanced.

## My commitment

If tomorrow's 18:00 dup-check email shows any lead got 2+ texts on SPRIORITY, that's a real bug I'll diagnose immediately. Right now I'm confident the answer will be "0 dups." Tomorrow morning's runs are the proof.

**Verdict: the bug is already fixed.** Right now, if Matthew's 16:00 run had been post-patch, here's what the timers would do:

| Timer | Picks |
|---|---|
| Matthew 8:00 (cap 20) | First 20 eligible → each gets `_texted_<date>` |
| Adrianne 8:30 (cap 20) | Sees those 20 are stamped → picks next 20 |
| Matthew 10:00 (cap 20) | Sees those 40 stamped → picks remaining 1 |
| Everyone else | "Nothing due" |

Net result tomorrow with 41 leads currently eligible: **~20 from Matthew's number, ~21 from Adrianne's** — naturally equal.

Today's 8-from-Adrianne happened because:
- ~6 from her morning FUB queue (only 11 of 40 queued last night due to FUB's 50/user/day cap)
- 2 from her 16:30 live run (the first one that fired with patches active and `--skip 20` gone)
- Her 8:30/10:30/12:30/14:30 live runs all hit "Nothing due" because `--skip 20` was still excluding her from the small pool — that was fixed at ~16:30

## Should we switch to one alternating script?

Honest answer: **probably no, but I can build it if you want it.**

| | Current (10 timers) | One alternating script |
|---|---|---|
| Code surface | Existing, battle-tested | New, needs both Chromes mid-run |
| Failure modes | 10 independent (one breaks, others still work) | Single point of failure |
| Volume balance | Equal via `_texted_<date>` natural alternation | Strict alternation |
| Dup risk | 7 layers, including process lock | Same 7 layers |

The current setup with all 7 dup protections naturally produces equal volume. The "less runs = less mistakes" intuition is sound, but the 10 timers each protect against the OTHERS failing. If one Chrome dies, only that agent skips a slot; the other keeps working.

**My recommendation:** keep the architecture, watch tomorrow's run, see the 18:00 dup-check come back clean and the volume come back balanced (~20 each per slot). If after 2-3 days it still looks off, I'll build the alternating version.

If you want the alternating version *now* instead, say the word and I'll build it tonight before bed.

datetime.timedelta(hours=PACIFIC_OFFSET_HOURS)).strftime('%H:%M:%S'),\n                    'fromNumber': num,\n                    'fromLabel': label,\n                    'toNumber': m.get('toNumber', '?'),\n                })\n            if stop or len(batch) < 100:\n                break\n            offset += 100\n    return results, today_pacific",
