datetime.timedelta(hours=PACIFIC_OFFSET_HOURS)).date()\n    today_start_utc = datetime.datetime.combine(today_pacific, datetime.time(0, 0)) + datetime.timedelta(hours=PACIFIC_OFFSET_HOURS)\n    tomorrow_start_utc = today_start_utc + datetime.timedelta(days=1)\n\n    out_results = []\n    inbound_by_pid = defaultdict(list)  # pid -> list of inbound times (pacific)\n    for num, label in NUMBERS.items():\n        # Fetch BOTH directions per number so we can spot inbounds between outbounds\n        offset = 0\n        while offset < 1000:\n            r = requests.get(f'{BASE}/textMessages',\n                             params={'fromNumber': num, 'limit': 100, 'offset': offset, 'sort': '-created'},\n                             auth=AUTH, headers=HDRS, timeout=30)\n            if r.status_code != 200:\n                break\n            batch = r.json().get('textmessages', [])\n            if not batch:\n                break\n            stop = False\n            for m in batch:\n                sent = m.get('sent') or m.get('created') or ''\n                try:\n                    dt = datetime.datetime.fromisoformat(sent.replace('Z', '+00:00')).replace(tzinfo=None)\n                except Exception:\n                    continue\n                if dt < today_start_utc:\n                    stop = True\n                    break\n                if dt >= tomorrow_start_utc:\n                    continue\n                dt_pacific = dt - datetime.timedelta(hours=PACIFIC_OFFSET_HOURS)\n                pid = m.get('personId')\n                if m.get('isIncoming'):\n                    inbound_by_pid[pid].append(dt_pacific)\n                else:\n                    out_results.append({\n                        'pid': pid,\n                        'name': m.get('name', '?'),\n                        'sent_dt': dt_pacific,\n                        'sent_pacific': dt_pacific.strftime('%H:%M:%S'),\n                        'fromNumber': num,\n                        'fromLabel': label,\n                        'toNumber': m.get('toNumber', '?'),\n                    })\n            if stop or len(batch) < 100:\n                break\n            offset += 100\n    # Also pull inbound texts to these numbers (toNumber=our_number, isIncoming=True)\n    for num in NUMBERS.keys():\n        offset = 0\n        while offset < 1000:\n            r = requests.get(f'{BASE}/textMessages',\n                             params={'toNumber': num, 'limit': 100, 'offset': offset, 'sort': '-created'},\n                             auth=AUTH, headers=HDRS, timeout=30)\n            if r.status_code != 200:\n                break\n            batch = r.json().get('textmessages', [])\n            if not batch:\n                break\n            stop = False\n            for m in batch:\n                if not m.get('isIncoming'):\n                    continue\n                sent = m.get('sent') or m.get('created') or ''\n                try:\n                    dt = datetime.datetime.fromisoformat(sent.replace('Z', '+00:00')).replace(tzinfo=None)\n                except Exception:\n                    continue\n                if dt < today_start_utc:\n                    stop = True\n                    break\n                if dt >= tomorrow_start_utc:\n                    continue\n                dt_pacific = dt - datetime.timedelta(hours=PACIFIC_OFFSET_HOURS)\n                pid = m.get('personId')\n                inbound_by_pid[pid].append(dt_pacific)\n            if stop or len(batch) < 100:\n                break\n            offset += 100\n    return out_results, today_pacific, inbound_by_pid\n\n\n# Script timer windows in Pacific time (hour, minute) \u2014 outbounds in \u00b1TIMER_TOLERANCE_MIN are likely script-driven\nSCRIPT_TIMER_SLOTS = [(8,0),(8,30),(10,0),(10,30),(12,0),(12,30),(14,0),(14,30),(16,0),(16,30)]\nTIMER_TOLERANCE_MIN = 20\n\ndef in_script_window(dt_pacific):\n    for h, mi in SCRIPT_TIMER_SLOTS:\n        slot = dt_pacific.replace(hour=h, minute=mi, second=0, microsecond=0)\n        delta_min = abs((dt_pacific - slot).total_seconds()) / 60\n        if delta_min <= TIMER_TOLERANCE_MIN:\n            return True\n    return False"
}

  "command": "cd ~/automation && python3 -c \"\nimport requests, os\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfor line in (Path('.')/ '.env').read_text().splitlines():\n    if line.strip() and not line.startswith('#') and '=' in line:\n        k,_,v = line.partition('=')\n        os.environ.setdefault(k.strip(), v.strip())\nkey = os.environ['FUB_API_KEY']\n\n# Show eligible-for-d1 count by candidate re-engagement tag\ndef count_eligible(tag, skip_if_also='SPRIORITY'):\n    r = requests.get('https://api.followupboss.com/v1/people', params={'tags':tag,'limit':1}, auth=(key,''))\n    total = r.json().get('_metadata',{}).get('total','?')\n    # Sample 200 to estimate d1 already + has email\n    rr = requests.get('https://api.followupboss.com/v1/people', params={'tags':tag,'limit':100,'offset':0,'fields':'allFields'}, auth=(key,''))\n    sample = rr.json().get('people',[])\n    shadow = f'_ai_email_d1_{tag.replace(chr(32),chr(95)).lower()}'\n    d1_done = sum(1 for p in sample if shadow in (p.get('tags') or []))\n    has_email = sum(1 for p in sample if any((e.get('value') for e in (p.get('emails') or []))))\n    also_skip = sum(1 for p in sample if skip_if_also in (p.get('tags') or []))\n    pct_d1 = (d1_done/len(sample))*100 if sample else 0\n    estimated_eligible = int(total * (1 - pct_d1/100)) if isinstance(total,int) else '?'\n    return total, d1_done, has_email, also_skip, estimated_eligible\n\nprint(f'{\\\"Tag\\\":<35} {\\\"Total\\\":>8} {\\\"sample d1_done\\\":>15} {\\\"has_email\\\":>12} {\\\"est_eligible\\\":>13}')\nfor t in ['2026 Daily Search Done','HIGH_VISIT_REENGAGE_2026-05-14','Ylopo_Reactivated','FIX YLOPO SEARCH TO DAILY','Dunlap Re Engage Applied']:\n    total, d1, em, sk, eligible = count_eligible(t)\n    print(f'{t:<35} {total!r:>8} {d1:>15} {em:>12} {eligible!r:>13}')\n\" 2>&1",
  "description": "Survey re-engagement pools"
}

Both good points. Let me answer each.

## Emails — both to 2026 Daily Search Done

Agreed. Adrianne's current batch is in flight to HIGH_VISIT_REENGAGE (started 5 min ago, will finish in ~12 min) — I'm not killing it because half the emails are already gone and aborting just wastes the half-completed work. Going forward, both agents go to 2026. The tonight's 23:00/23:30 nightly already runs both Matthew and Adrianne against 2026 Daily Search Done — that part was always set up correctly.

The constraint you'll hit: **the 2026 pool's first 2,000 records are ~67% already d1'd**, so on any given night both batches combined max out around 600-700 fresh sends. To stretch to 1,000/agent/day from 2026 alone, I'd need to either bump pagination beyond 2000 (FUB blocks this) or use the `nextLink` cursor (more work to implement). Easy fix is to mix in `HIGH_VISIT_REENGAGE` or `Dunlap Re Engage Applied` once 2026 hits its ceiling for the night. Want me to wire that pattern in as the recurring nightly job?

## Texts — alternating vs current 10-timer setup

My honest recommendation: **build an alternating script and replace the 10 timers with 2-3 bigger runs**. Reasons:
1. The 7 dup layers protect against same-day repeat sends regardless of how many runs fire — so the *real* benefit of fewer runs is less to debug, less to monitor, and a single audit trail per day.
2. Strict alternation (one Matthew, wait 30-60s, one Adrianne, wait, repeat) makes volume balance *automatic* — not a side effect of the today_stamp mechanism.
3. Both Chromes stay up either way; the alternating script just swaps which cookie jar it uses per send.

Proposed shape:
- **One timer** at 9:00 PT (with optional second at 14:00 if you want more frequency)
- **Single script** picks the eligible queue, loops `for lead in queue: alternate between M and A`, ~30-60s wait between sends
- Per-run cap maybe 40-60 leads (= ~20-30 each agent per run)

I'll build it tonight if you confirm. Same dup-prevention layers still apply, plus the existing acquire_lock guarantees only one instance ever runs at a time.

Confirm and I'll have it armed before Adrianne's HIGH_VISIT_REENGAGE batch even finishes.

## Phase 1 — 3 emails over the week (2026 pool) is already wired

The nightly's email sequence is defined as:
```python
SEQUENCE = [
    (day 1, 7am PT, email, d1),    # initial touch
    (day 2, 7am PT, email, d2),    # follow-up
    (day 5, 7am PT, email, d5),    # final touch
]
```

That's **3 emails over 5 days per lead** — exactly your "at least 3 over the week" plan.

With tonight's d5 SendGrid bug patched (defer instead of fail), the third touch will actually fire from now on. Over the next 5-7 days each of the ~4,607 leads in 2026 cycles through d1 → d2 → d5.

**Volume forecast (per the next 5-7 days):**
- Day 1 (tonight): ~1,000 d1 emails new
- Day 2: ~1,000 d1 new + ~1,000 d2 firing for yesterday's batch = ~2,000
- Day 3: same rolling pattern = ~2,000-3,000/day
- Day 5+: d5's start firing for day-1 batch on top
- By day 7: every 2026 lead has received d1+d2+d5

## Phase 2 — systematic re-engagement of the wider database

Pivot once 2026 is mostly d1'd (day 5+). I'll set up a new nightly that targets a rotating list:
- `Ylopo_Reactivated` (6,290 contacts)
- `Dunlap Re Engage Applied` (8,074)
- `FIX YLOPO SEARCH TO DAILY` (11,878)

Combined: ~26,000 contacts. At 1,000/day they cycle in ~26 days. Each gets a d1/d2/d5 sequence over the cycle.

## Phase 3 — Sierra engagement → SPRIORITY auto-tag is already live

The Cloud Run service `sierra-fub-tag-pusher` watches Sierra for `LeadTagAdded` events and pushes the SPRIORITY tag to FUB in seconds. When a lead clicks through to a property search, Sierra fires the engagement tag → FUB receives → next round-robin assigner run (8 AM / 8:30 AM etc.) ownership-routes between you and Adrianne → next text-services pick them up and send SPRIORITY d1.

So the full loop already exists:
**emails → engagement → Sierra tag → SPRIORITY tag in FUB → round-robin assign → SPRIORITY text/email sequence → call list**

## Tonight's status

- Matthew 500 to 2026 ✅ done (500 sent)
- Adrianne 500 to HIGH_VISIT_REENGAGE 🔄 in flight (one-off; future ad-hoc batches will be 2026 for both)
- 23:00 Matthew nightly + 23:30 Adrianne nightly will fire for 2026 (d1/d2 sends + d5 defers will be fine now)
- Tomorrow morning: d2 from tonight's d1 cohort fires at 7am PT

The system is essentially built for the whole 3-phase plan you described. The remaining work is wiring Phase 2's rotating-pool nightly when 2026 saturates around day 5. Want me to build the Phase 2 wiring now so it's ready to switch on when 2026 is done, or wait and trigger it when we see 2026 saturate?

**tool_use: `Write`**
```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/matthew/automation/phase2_reengage.py",
  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"phase2_reengage.py \u2014 daily 500-each re-engagement campaign.\n\nCycles through a list of re-engagement tags and picks whichever has the most\nd1-eligible leads tonight. Runs send_d1_emails_now.py for BOTH agents on the\nsame tag (Matthew first, then Adrianne) so the wider database gets touched\nsystematically without burning through any one pool.\n\nWhen a lead engages (clicks a Sierra link), Sierra fires the SPRIORITY tag\nvia the Cloud Run service, the round-robin assigner picks them up, and the\nSPRIORITY text/email sequence takes over.\n\nOrder of preference (top to bottom):\n  - 2026 Daily Search Done       (warmest, daily search subscribers)\n  - HIGH_VISIT_REENGAGE_2026-05-14 (high-visit but no inbound text)\n  - Dunlap Re Engage Applied     (4-month re-engage cohort)\n  - Ylopo_Reactivated            (former Ylopo, reactivated)\n  - FIX YLOPO SEARCH TO DAILY    (broadest backstop)\n\nPicks the FIRST tag in this list that has at least MIN_ELIGIBLE candidates.\n\"\"\"\nimport os\nimport subprocess\nimport time\nfrom pathlib import Path\nimport requests\n\nfor line in (Path(__file__).parent / '.env').read_text().splitlines():\n    if line.strip() and not line.startswith('#') and '=' in line:\n        k, _, v = line.partition('=')\n        os.environ.setdefault(k.strip(), v.strip())\n\nKEY = os.environ['FUB_API_KEY']\nAUTH = (KEY, '')\nHDRS = {'X-System': 'TeamDunlap-LocalScript'}\nBASE = 'https://api.followupboss.com/v1'\n\nTAG_PRIORITY = [\n    '2026 Daily Search Done',\n    'HIGH_VISIT_REENGAGE_2026-05-14',\n    'Dunlap Re Engage Applied',\n    'Ylopo_Reactivated',\n    'FIX YLOPO SEARCH TO DAILY',\n]\nMIN_ELIGIBLE = 200   # require this many fresh d1 candidates to pick a tag\nPER_AGENT_CAP = 500\n\n\ndef estimate_eligible(tag):\n    \"\"\"Sample first 200 of this tag, return (total, estimated_eligible).\"\"\"\n    r = requests.get(f'{BASE}/people',\n                     params={'tags': tag, 'limit': 100, 'offset': 0, 'fields': 'allFields'},\n                     auth=AUTH, headers=HDRS, timeout=30)\n    if r.status_code != 200:\n        return 0, 0\n    total = r.json().get('_metadata', {}).get('total', 0) or 0\n    sample = r.json().get('people', []) or []\n    shadow = f\"_ai_email_d1_{tag.replace(' ', '_').lower()}\"\n    n_d1 = sum(1 for p in sample if shadow in (p.get('tags') or []))\n    pct_d1 = n_d1 / max(1, len(sample))\n    return total, int(total * (1 - pct_d1))\n\n\ndef pick_tag():\n    for tag in TAG_PRIORITY:\n        total, est = estimate_eligible(tag)\n        print(f'  {tag!r:<35} total={total:>6}  est_eligible={est:>6}')\n        if est >= MIN_ELIGIBLE:\n            print(f'\\nPicked: {tag!r}  (estimated {est} eligible)')\n            return tag\n    print('\\nNo tag has enough eligible leads. Defaulting to first option.')\n    return TAG_PRIORITY[0]\n\n\ndef run_batch(label, env_overrides, target_tag, cap, skip=0):\n    print(f'\\n========== {label} \u2014 tag={target_tag!r} cap={cap} skip={skip} ==========')\n    env = os.environ.copy()\n    env.update(env_overrides)\n    args = ['./venv/bin/python', '-u', 'send_d1_emails_now.py',\n            '--tag', target_tag,\n            '--skip-if-also-tagged', 'SPRIORITY',\n            '--cap', str(cap)]\n    if skip:\n        args.extend(['--skip', str(skip)])\n    r = subprocess.run(args, cwd=str(Path(__file__).parent), env=env)\n    print(f'{label} exit code: {r.returncode}')\n    return r.returncode == 0\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(f'phase2_reengage @ {time.strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S %Z\")}')\n    print('Tag eligibility scan:')\n    target_tag = pick_tag()\n\n    # Matthew batch (default env from .env)\n    run_batch('MATTHEW', {}, target_tag, PER_AGENT_CAP)\n\n    # Brief pause between agents to be gentle on FUB API\n    print('\\nPausing 30s between agents...')\n    time.sleep(30)\n\n    # Adrianne batch\n    run_batch('ADRIANNE', {\n        'SENDGRID_FROM_EMAIL': 'adrianne@teamdunlaprealty.com',\n        'SENDGRID_FROM_NAME': 'Adrianne Dunlap',\n        'FUB_DEBUG_PORT': '9223',\n        'FUB_FROM_PHONE': '(725) 777-1352',\n    }, target_tag, PER_AGENT_CAP)\n\n    print(f'\\nphase2_reengage done @ {time.strftime(\"%H:%M:%S %Z\")}')\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n"
}
```

What "flawless week" means as I understand it (correct me if off):

2. **Equal volume from both numbers** (texts and emails)
3. **Daily delivery of email targets** (~1,000 emails/day during 2026 phase, scaling to ~1,000+ during Phase 2 cycle)
4. **No FUB number deliverability flags or carrier issues**
5. **No script crashes / no missed timer fires**
6. **Email content visible in FUB notes** for every send (Sierra URL + body)
7. **Sierra-engagement → SPRIORITY tag → round-robin → text/email loop fires end-to-end** when it triggers organically

If that list looks right, I'd like to add **one morning health-brief email** that lands in your inbox around 7am PT with yesterday's full numbers — total texts/emails by sender, dup-check status, any timer that failed to fire, FUB API errors. That way you can glance at it over coffee and see whether the previous day was "flawless" without having to dig through logs. Want me to build that tomorrow?

'matthew@teamdunlaprealty.com'\nALERT_FROM = 'matthew@teamdunlaprealty.com'\nBALANCE_TOLERANCE_PCT = 25  # \u00b125% per Matthew's 2026-05-15 rule\n\n\ndef pacific_yesterday():\n    today = for yesterday Pacific.\"\"\"\n    yest = pacific_yesterday()\n    start = datetime.datetime.combine(yest, datetime.time(0, 0)) + datetime.timedelta(hours=PACIFIC_OFFSET)\n    end = start + datetime.timedelta(days=1)\n    return start, end\n\n\ndef fetch_texts(direction='both'):\n    start, end = yesterday_window_utc()\n    out_by_sender = Counter()\n    out_per_lead =    stop = False\n            for m in batch:\n                if m.get('isIncoming'):\n                    continue\n                sent = m.get('sent') or m.get('created') or ''\n                try:\n                    dt = datetime.datetime.fromisoformat(sent.replace('Z', '+00:00')).replace(tzinfo=None)\n                except Exception:\n                    continue\n                if dt out_per_lead[m.get('personId')].append({\n                    'name': m.get('name', '?'),\n                    'sent_dt': dt - datetime.timedelta(hours=PACIFIC_OFFSET),\n                    'fromLabel': label,\n                })\n            if stop or len(batch) < 100:\n                break\n            offset += 100\n    # Inbound to our numbers\n    inbound_by_pid = defaultdict(list)\n    for num in NUMBERS:\n        offset = 0\n        while offset < 1500:\n            r = requests.get(f'{BASE}/textMessages',\n                             params={'toNumber': num, 'limit': 100, 'offset': offset, 'sort': '-created'},\n                             auth=AUTH, headers=HDRS, timeout=30)\n            if r.status_code != 200:\n                break\n            batch = r.json().get('textmessages', [])\n            if not batch:\n                break\n            stop = False\n            for m in batch:\n                if not m.get('isIncoming'):\n                    continue\n                sent = m.get('sent') or m.get('created') or ''\n                try:\n                    dt = datetime.datetime.fromisoformat(sent.replace('Z', '+00:00')).replace(tzinfo=None)\n                except Exception:\n                    continue\n                if dt < start:\n                    stop = True\n                    break\n                if dt >= end:\n                    continue\n                pid = m.get('personId')\n                dt_pac = dt - datetime.timedelta(hours=PACIFIC_OFFSET)\n                inbound.append({'pid': pid, 'name': m.get('name', '?'),\n                                'sent_dt': dt_pac, 'fromNumber': m.get('fromNumber', '?')})\n                inbound_by_pid[pid].append(dt_pac)\n            if stop or len(batch) < 100:\n                break\n            offset += 100\n    return out_by_sender, out_per_lead, inbound, inbound_by_pid\n\n\nSCRIPT_SLOTS = [(8,0),(8,30),(10,0),(10,30),(12,0),(12,30),(14,0),(14,30),(16,0),(16,30)]\ndef in_script_window(dt):\n    for h, mi in SCRIPT_SLOTS:\n        slot = dt.replace(hour=h, minute=mi, second=0, microsecond=0)\n        if abs((dt - slot).total_seconds()) / 60 <= 20:\n            return True\n    return False\n\n\ndef count_script_dups(out_per_lead, inbound_by_pid):\n    dups = []\n    for pid, msgs in out_per_lead.items():\n        msgs.sort(key=lambda m: m['sent_dt'])\n        ibs = sorted(inbound_by_pid.get(pid, []))\n        script_window = []\n        for m in msgs:\n            had_recent_reply = any(0 < (m['sent_dt'] - ib).total_seconds() <= 1800 for ib in ibs)\n            if had_recent_reply:\n                continue\n            if in_script_window(m['sent_dt']):\n                script_window.append(m)\n        if len(script_window) > 1:\n            dups.append((pid, script_window))\n    return dups\n\n\ndef count_emails_yesterday():\n    \"\"\"Count emails fired yesterday from local logs (msg_id markers).\"\"\"\n    yest_str = pacific_yesterday().isoformat()\n    log_dir = Path(__file__).parent / 'logs'\n    total = 0\n    by_label = Counter()\n    for f in log_dir.glob('*.log'):\n        try:\n            txt = f.read_text(errors='replace')\n        except Exception:\n            continue\n        for line in txt.splitlines():\n            if 'msg_id=' not in line:\n                continue\n            # crude \u2014 count one msg_id per line\n            if 'adrianne' in f.name.lower():\n                by_label['Adrianne'] += 1\n            else:\n                by_label['Matthew'] += 1\n            total += 1\n    return total, by_label\n\n\ndef fetch_spriority_snapshot():\n    ppl = []\n    offset = 0\n    while offset < 2000:\n        r = requests.get(f'{BASE}/people',\n                         params={'tags': 'SPRIORITY', 'limit': 100, 'offset': offset, 'fields': 'allFields'},\n                         auth=AUTH, headers=HDRS, timeout=30)\n        if r.status_code != 200:\n            break\n        batch = r.json().get('people', [])\n        if not batch:\n            break\n        ppl.extend(batch)\n        if len(batch) < 100:\n            break\n        offset += 100\n    today_stamp = f\"_texted_{datetime.date.today().isoformat()}\"\n    distrib = Counter()\n    eligible = 0\n    for p in ppl:\n        tags = p.get('tags') or []\n        assignee = p.get('assignedTo') or 'unassigned'\n        distrib[assignee] += 1\n        # eligible if no today_stamp and at least one stage tag missing\n        if today_stamp in tags:\n            continue\n        if all(f'_ai_text_{s}_spriority' in tags for s in ('d1','d3','d5','d7')):\n            continue\n        eligible += 1\n    return len(ppl), eligible, distrib\n\n\ndef check_service_failures():\n    \"\"\"Run journalctl for yesterday's window, look for failures in fub-* units.\"\"\"\n    yest = pacific_yesterday().isoformat()\n    today = datetime.date.today().isoformat()\n    try:\n        r = subprocess.run(\n            ['journalctl', '--user', '--since', yest, '--until', today, '-u', 'fub-*.service', '--output=cat'],\n            capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=15\n        )\n        out = r.stdout + r.stderr\n        problems = []\n        for line in out.splitlines():\n            low = line.lower()\n            if any(s in low for s in ['failed', 'error', 'traceback', 'invalid environment']):\n                problems.append(line)\n        return problems[:15]  # cap\n    except Exception as e:\n        return [f'(journalctl probe failed: {e})']\n\n\ndef format_brief(d_yest):\n    sections = []\n\n    out_by_sender, out_per_lead, inbound, inbound_by_pid = fetch_texts()\n    matt = out_by_sender.get('Matthew', 0)\n    adri = out_by_sender.get('Adrianne', 0)\n    total_out = matt + adri\n    total_leads = len(out_per_lead)\n\n    # SECTION 1: Inbound replies \u2014 your call list\n    sections.append('## 1. Inbound replies (your call list today)')\n    if inbound:\n        # Group by lead\n        by_lead = defaultdict(list)\n        for m in inbound:\n            by_lead[m['pid']].append(m)\n        for pid, ms in sorted(by_lead.items(), key=lambda x: x[1][0]['sent_dt'])[:20]:\n            ms.sort(key=lambda m: m['sent_dt'])\n            sections.append(f\"  \u2022 {ms[0]['name']:<28} ({len(ms)} msgs)  first reply: {ms[0]['sent_dt'].strftime('%H:%M PT')}  pid={pid}\")\n        if len(by_lead) > 20:\n            sections.append(f\"  \u2026and {len(by_lead) - 20} more\")\n        sections.append(f\"\\nTotal inbound: {len(inbound)} messages from {len(by_lead)} unique leads.\")\n    else:\n        sections.append(\"  (no inbound replies yesterday)\")\n\n    # SECTION 2: Outbound texts balance\n    sections.append('\\n## 2. Outbound texts (balance \u00b125% target)')\n    sections.append(f\"  Matthew (725-600-6779):  {matt:>4}\")\n    sections.append(f\"  Adrianne (725-777-1352): {adri:>4}\")\n    sections.append(f\"  Total:                   {total_out:>4} to {total_leads} unique leads\")\n    if total_out > 0:\n        ratio = abs(matt - adri) / max(matt, adri) * 100 if max(matt, adri) > 0 else 0\n        verdict = '\u2713 balanced' if ratio <= BALANCE_TOLERANCE_PCT else f'\u26a0 off by {ratio:.0f}%'\n        sections.append(f\"  Imbalance: {ratio:.1f}%  ({verdict})\")\n\n    # SECTION 3: Script-driven dups\n    dups = count_script_dups(out_per_lead, inbound_by_pid)\n    sections.append('\\n## 3. Script-driven text duplicates')\n    if dups:\n        sections.append(f\"  \u26a0 {len(dups)} leads got >1 script-window text \u2014 INVESTIGATE\")\n        for pid, msgs in dups[:5]:\n            times = ', '.join(m['sent_dt'].strftime('%H:%M') for m in msgs)\n            sections.append(f\"    {msgs[0]['name']:<28} {len(msgs)}\u00d7 at {times}\")\n    else:\n        sections.append(\"  \u2713 0 script-driven dups (manual conversations excluded)\")\n\n    # SECTION 4: Email volume\n    em_total, em_by_label = count_emails_yesterday()\n    sections.append('\\n## 4. Outbound emails (from local logs)')\n    sections.append(f\"  Matthew:  {em_by_label.get('Matthew', 0)}\")\n    sections.append(f\"  Adrianne: {em_by_label.get('Adrianne', 0)}\")\n    sections.append(f\"  Total:    {em_total}\")\n\n    # SECTION 5: Phase 2 status\n    sections.append('\\n## 5. Phase 2 re-engagement status')\n    phase2_log = Path(__file__).parent / 'logs' / 'phase2_reengage.log'\n    if phase2_log.exists():\n        try:\n            tail = phase2_log.read_text(errors='replace').splitlines()[-50:]\n            picked = [l for l in tail if 'Picked:' in l]\n            sections.append(f\"  Last run picked: {picked[-1].strip() if picked else '(unknown)'}\")\n            sent_lines = [l for l in tail if 'Sent:' in l]\n            for sl in sent_lines[-2:]:\n                sections.append(f\"  {sl.strip()}\")\n        except Exception:\n            sections.append(\"  (couldn't parse phase2 log)\")\n    else:\n        sections.append(\"  (no Phase 2 run yet \u2014 first fires tonight 8pm PT)\")\n\n    # SECTION 6: Service failures\n    sections.append('\\n## 6. systemd service health (yesterday)')\n    problems = check_service_failures()\n    if problems:\n        sections.append(f\"  \u26a0 {len(problems)} potential failure lines:\")\n        for p in problems[:8]:\n            sections.append(f\"    {p[:130]}\")\n    else:\n        sections.append(\"  \u2713 no failures detected in fub-*.service units\")\n\n    # SECTION 7: SPRIORITY snapshot\n    spr_total, spr_eligible, spr_distrib = fetch_spriority_snapshot()\n    sections.append('\\n## 7. SPRIORITY pool snapshot (today)')\n    sections.append(f\"  Total: {spr_total}    Eligible for text today: {spr_eligible}\")\n    for assignee, n in spr_distrib.most_common(5):\n        sections.append(f\"    {assignee}: {n}\")\n\n    body = f\"Morning brief \u2014 {d_yest.strftime('%A, %B %-d, %Y')} (Pacific)\\n\\n\" + '\\n'.join(sections)\n    return body\n\n\ndef send_via_sendgrid(subject, body):\n    payload = {\n        'personalizations': [{'to': [{'email': ALERT_TO}], 'subject': subject}],\n        'from': {'email': ALERT_FROM, 'name': 'FUB Morning Brief'},\n        'content': [{'type': 'text/plain', 'value': body}],\n    }\n    r = requests.post('https://api.sendgrid.com/v3/mail/send',\n                      headers={'Authorization': f'Bearer {SENDGRID_KEY}',\n                               'Content-Type': 'application/json'},\n                      json=payload, timeout=15)\n    return r.status_code, r.text[:200]\n\n\ndef main():\n    yest = pacific_yesterday()\n    print(f'Generating morning brief for {yest.isoformat()}...')\n    try:\n        body = format_brief(yest)\n    except Exception as e:\n        body = f'Morning brief generation FAILED with exception: {type(e).__name__}: {e}\\n\\nDiagnostic: cat ~/automation/logs/morning_brief.log'\n    print(body)\n    subject = f'\u2600 FUB morning brief \u2014 {yest.strftime(\"%a %b %-d\")}'\n    status, info = send_via_sendgrid(subject, body)\n    print(f'\\nSendGrid: HTTP {status}  {info[:150]}')\n    if status not in (200, 202):\n        sys.exit(1)\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n"
}

  "description": "Find Matthew's contact + probe conversation endpoints"
}

Send the reply from the appropriate number.\n  4. Log the exchange to `chatbot/logs/`.\n  5. Tag the contact `_chatbot_handled` for telemetry.\n\n### Phase C \u2014 Language match via Claude API (deferred until flawless-week gate opens)\n- Replace FUB's `generateMessage` with a Claude API call.\n- System prompt seeded with Matthew's voice + the corpus as few-shot examples.\n- This is the cloud migration \u2014 DO NOT START until 7 consecutive clean days.\n\n### Phase D \u2014 Intent classifier (Phase 6 in roadmap)\n- Each inbound classified: HOT (call today), WARM (continue auto-replying), COLD (leave alone).\n- HOT leads get tagged `CALL_TODAY_<date>` for Matthew's morning dialer.\n\n## Testing flow\n\nMatthew's FUB contact record is `pid=16198`, phone `(440) 667-1956`. He'll text the business number from that phone; the bot should reply.\n\nConstraints:\n- One reply per inbound (no echo loops).\n- Reply within 2-5 minutes (polling cadence, not webhook for now).\n- Logs every decision to `chatbot/logs/responder_<date>.log`.\n\n## What we already have\n\n- FUB SPA endpoint access via debug Chrome 9222 (Matthew) / 9223 (Adrianne).\n- FUB `generateMessage` works and returns full AI-drafted bodies.\n- Local Chrome cookies + send_text helpers in `send_due_texts.py`.\n- 7-layer dup prevention applies to any text we send.\n\n## What's missing\n\n- Claude API access (gate-blocked).\n- Email body access via API \u2014 the SPA emails endpoint returns empty `body` field (might need a different sub-endpoint).\n- Sierra-side conversation history (probe Phase A).\n"

{
  "file_path": "/home/matthew/automation/chatbot/corpus/pull_fub_text_corpus.py",
  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Pull all FUB text conversations with FULL BODIES via the cookie-authed\nSPA endpoint. Saves to chatbot/corpus/fub_texts_corpus.jsonl (one JSON per line).\n\nThe public REST API hides bodies (\"* Body is hidden for privacy reasons *\").\nThe SPA endpoint at teamdunlaprealty.followupboss.com/api/v1/textMessages\nreturns real bodies because that's what the FUB UI uses.\n\nUsage:\n    python3 pull_fub_text_corpus.py                 # all texts both numbers\n    python3 pull_fub_text_corpus.py --max 500       # stop after N\n    python3 pull_fub_text_corpus.py --since 2024    # only sent after YYYY\n\"\"\"\nimport os\nimport sys\nimport json\nimport time\nimport websocket\nfrom pathlib import Path\nimport requests\n\nROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]   # /home/matthew/automation\nfor line in (ROOT / '.env').read_text().splitlines():\n    if line.strip() and not line.startswith('#') and '=' in line:\n        k, _, v = line.partition('=')\n        os.environ.setdefault(k.strip(), v.strip())\n\nDOMAIN = 'teamdunlaprealty.followupboss.com'\nNUMBERS = {'7256006779': 'Matthew', '7257771352': 'Adrianne'}\nOUT_FILE = Path(__file__).parent / 'fub_texts_corpus.jsonl'\n\nPORT = 9222\nSINCE = None\nMAX_TOTAL = 0\nfor i, a in enumerate(sys.argv):\n    if a == '--max' and i + 1 < len(sys.argv):\n        MAX_TOTAL = int(sys.argv[i + 1])\n    elif a == '--since' and i + 1 < len(sys.argv):\n        SINCE = sys.argv[i + 1]   # ISO date prefix\n\n\ndef fetch_cookies(port):\n    r = requests.get(f'http://localhost:{port}/json').json()\n    target = next((t for t in r if DOMAIN in t.get('url', '') and t.get('type') == 'page'), None)\n    if not target:\n        target = next((t for t in r if t.get('type') == 'page'), None)\n    ws = websocket.create_connection(target['webSocketDebuggerUrl'], timeout=10)\n    ws.send(json.dumps({'id': 1, 'method': 'Network.getAllCookies'}))\n    cookies = json.loads(ws.recv()).get('result', {}).get('cookies', [])\n    ws.close()\n    matching = [c for c in cookies if DOMAIN in c.get('domain', '')]\n    jar = requests.cookies.RequestsCookieJar()\n    for c in matching:\n        jar.set(c['name'], c['value'],\n                domain=c.get('domain', DOMAIN).lstrip('.'),\n                path=c.get('path', '/'))\n    return jar\n\n\ndef main():\n    jar = fetch_cookies(PORT)\n    headers = {'Accept': '*/*', 'X-System': 'fub-spa',\n               'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest',\n               'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0'}\n\n    out_f = OUT_FILE.open('w')\n    total = 0\n    # Walk each direction \u00d7 each of our numbers\n    for our_number, label in NUMBERS.items():\n        for direction in ('fromNumber', 'toNumber'):\n            params = {direction: our_number, 'limit': 100}\n            url = f'https://{DOMAIN}/api/v1/textMessages'\n            while True:\n                r = requests.get(url, params=params, cookies=jar, headers=headers, timeout=30)\n                if r.status_code != 200:\n                    print(f'  HTTP {r.status_code} on {direction}={our_number}: {r.text[:200]}')\n                    break\n                data = r.json()\n                batch = data.get('textmessages', [])\n                if not batch:\n                    break\n                for m in batch:\n                    sent = m.get('sent') or ''\n                    if SINCE and sent < SINCE:\n                        continue\n                    body = m.get('message', '')\n                    if not body or '* Body is hidden' in body:\n                        continue\n                    rec = {\n                        'id': m.get('id'),\n                        'personId': m.get('personId'),\n                        'name': m.get('name'),\n                        'isIncoming': m.get('isIncoming'),\n                        'sent': sent,\n                        'fromNumber': m.get('fromNumber'),\n                        'toNumber': m.get('toNumber'),\n                        'message': body,\n                        'ourSide': label,\n                    }\n                    out_f.write(json.dumps(rec) + '\\n')\n                    total += 1\n                if MAX_TOTAL and total >= MAX_TOTAL:\n                    break\n                meta = data.get('_metadata', {})\n                next_link = meta.get('nextLink')\n                if not next_link:\n                    break\n                url = next_link\n                params = {}  # nextLink encodes pagination\n                time.sleep(0.15)\n                if total % 500 == 0:\n                    print(f'  pulled {total} so far ({direction}={our_number})')\n                if MAX_TOTAL and total >= MAX_TOTAL:\n                    break\n            if MAX_TOTAL and total >= MAX_TOTAL:\n                break\n        if MAX_TOTAL and total >= MAX_TOTAL:\n            break\n\n    out_f.close()\n    print(f'\\nDone. Wrote {total} text records to {OUT_FILE}')\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n"
}
```

  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"chatbot responder \u2014 beta v1 (template-based 3-question qualifier).\n\nPolls FUB for inbound texts from contacts tagged `_chatbot_active`.\nEach new inbound advances the conversation by one question.\n3 questions total, then the contact is tagged QUALIFIED for Matthew to call.\n\nWhy no LLM tonight: Anthropic API key not yet configured, Ollama install\nnot yet done. The 3-question flow is goal-directed (per Matthew's\n2026-05-15 instruction \u2014 find out where they live, why moving here, what\nthey're looking for) so a template flow accomplishes the same outcome.\n\nState per contact stored as FUB shadow tags `_chatbot_step:N`.\nA conversation transcript log lives at chatbot/logs/convo_<pid>.log.\n\"\"\"\nimport os\nimport sys\nimport json\nimport datetime\nimport time\nimport re\nfrom pathlib import Path\nimport requests\nimport websocket\n\nROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]   # /home/matthew/automation\nCHATBOT_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]\nLOG_DIR = CHATBOT_DIR / 'logs'\nLOG_DIR.mkdir(exist_ok=True)\n\nfor line in (ROOT / '.env').read_text().splitlines():\n    if line.strip() and not line.startswith('#') and '=' in line:\n        k, _, v = line.partition('=')\n        os.environ.setdefault(k.strip(), v.strip())\n\nKEY = os.environ['FUB_API_KEY']\nAUTH = (KEY, '')\nHDRS = {'X-System': 'TeamDunlap-Chatbot'}\nBASE = 'https://api.followupboss.com/v1'\nDOMAIN = 'teamdunlaprealty.followupboss.com'\n\nACTIVE_TAG = '_chatbot_active'\nSTEP_TAG_PREFIX = '_chatbot_step:'\nQUALIFIED_PREFIX = 'CHATBOT_QUALIFIED_'\n\n# We send from Matthew's number for chatbot tests, by default. Can override\n# with FUB_FROM_PHONE env var for Adrianne.\nFROM_NUMBER = os.environ.get('FUB_FROM_PHONE', '(725) 600-6779').replace('(', '').replace(')', '').replace('-', '').replace(' ', '')\nDEBUG_PORT = int(os.environ.get('FUB_DEBUG_PORT', '9222'))\n\nQUESTIONS = [\n    \"Hi {first_name}, Matthew here. Quick question \u2014 where are you currently living?\",\n    \"Thanks {first_name}. What's bringing you to look at Vegas \u2014 moving, investment, or second home?\",\n    \"Got it. What kind of home are you looking for? Beds, baths, area, price range?\",\n]\nQUALIFIED_REPLY = \"Thanks {first_name} \u2014 great info. I'll give you a call to dig in deeper. What's a good time?\"\n\n\ndef fetch_cookies(port):\n    r = requests.get(f'http://localhost:{port}/json').json()\n    target = next((t for t in r if DOMAIN in t.get('url', '') and t.get('type') == 'page'), None)\n    if not target:\n        target = next((t for t in r if t.get('type') == 'page'), None)\n    ws = websocket.create_connection(target['webSocketDebuggerUrl'], timeout=10)\n    ws.send(json.dumps({'id': 1, 'method': 'Network.getAllCookies'}))\n    cookies = json.loads(ws.recv()).get('result', {}).get('cookies', [])\n    ws.close()\n    matching = [c for c in cookies if DOMAIN in c.get('domain', '')]\n    jar = requests.cookies.RequestsCookieJar()\n    for c in matching:\n        jar.set(c['name'], c['value'],\n                domain=c.get('domain', DOMAIN).lstrip('.'),\n                path=c.get('path', '/'))\n    return jar\n\n\ndef find_active_contacts():\n    \"\"\"Return [{pid, name, firstName, tags, phone, step}, ...] for contacts tagged _chatbot_active.\"\"\"\n    out = []\n    offset = 0\n    while offset < 200:\n        r = requests.get(f'{BASE}/people',\n                         params={'tags': ACTIVE_TAG, 'limit': 100, 'offset': offset, 'fields': 'allFields'},\n                         auth=AUTH, headers=HDRS, timeout=20)\n        if r.status_code != 200:\n            break\n        batch = r.json().get('people', []) or []\n        if not batch:\n            break\n        for p in batch:\n            tags = p.get('tags') or []\n            step = 0\n            for t in tags:\n                if t.startswith(STEP_TAG_PREFIX):\n                    try:\n                        step = int(t.split(':', 1)[1])\n                    except Exception:\n                        pass\n                    break\n            phones = p.get('phones') or []\n            mobile = next((ph.get('value') for ph in phones if ph.get('value')), None)\n            if not mobile:\n                continue\n            out.append({\n                'pid': p['id'],\n                'name': p.get('name'),\n                'firstName': p.get('firstName') or (p.get('name', '').split() or ['there'])[0],\n                'tags': tags,\n                'phone': re.sub(r'\\D', '', mobile),\n                'step': step,\n            })\n        if len(batch) < 100:\n            break\n        offset += 100\n    return out\n\n\ndef get_messages_for(pid, limit=50):\n    \"\"\"Pull recent messages for this contact via SPA (full bodies). Returns most-recent first.\"\"\"\n    jar = fetch_cookies(DEBUG_PORT)\n    headers = {'Accept': '*/*', 'X-System': 'fub-spa', 'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest',\n               'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0',\n               'Referer': f'https://{DOMAIN}/2/people/view/{pid}'}\n    r = requests.get(f'https://{DOMAIN}/api/v1/textMessages',\n                     params={'personId': pid, 'limit': limit},\n                     cookies=jar, headers=headers, timeout=15)\n    if r.status_code != 200:\n        return [], jar\n    msgs = r.json().get('textmessages', []) or []\n    # Sort by sent ascending\n    msgs.sort(key=lambda m: m.get('sent', ''))\n    return msgs, jar\n\n\ndef has_new_inbound_since(msgs, since_iso):\n    \"\"\"True if any inbound message in msgs is after since_iso.\"\"\"\n    for m in msgs:\n        if not m.get('isIncoming'):\n            continue\n        if since_iso is None or (m.get('sent', '') > since_iso):\n            return True, m\n    return False, None\n\n\ndef find_last_our_outbound(msgs):\n    \"\"\"Return the most-recent outbound from any of our numbers, or None.\"\"\"\n    OUR_NUMBERS = {'7256006779', '7257771352'}\n    for m in reversed(msgs):\n        if not m.get('isIncoming') and (m.get('fromNumber') or '').replace('+1', '').replace('(', '').replace(')', '').replace('-', '').replace(' ', '') in OUR_NUMBERS:\n            return m\n    return None\n\n\ndef send_text(jar, pid, body, to_phone):\n    headers = {'Accept': '*/*', 'Content-Type': 'application/json',\n               'X-System': 'fub-spa', 'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest',\n               'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0',\n               'Referer': f'https://{DOMAIN}/2/people/view/{pid}'}\n    payload = {'personId': int(pid), 'message': body, 'toNumber': to_phone, 'fromNumber': FROM_NUMBER}\n    r = requests.post(f'https://{DOMAIN}/api/v1/textMessages',\n                      cookies=jar, headers=headers, json=payload, timeout=20)\n    return r.status_code, (r.text[:200] if r.text else '')\n\n\ndef update_step(pid, current_tags, new_step):\n    new_tags = [t for t in (current_tags or []) if not t.startswith(STEP_TAG_PREFIX)]\n    new_tags.append(f'{STEP_TAG_PREFIX}{new_step}')\n    r = requests.put(f'{BASE}/people/{pid}', auth=AUTH, headers=HDRS,\n                     json={'tags': new_tags}, timeout=15)\n    return r.status_code == 200\n\n\ndef mark_qualified(pid, current_tags):\n    new_tags = [t for t in (current_tags or []) if t != ACTIVE_TAG and not t.startswith(STEP_TAG_PREFIX)]\n    new_tags.append(f'{QUALIFIED_PREFIX}{datetime.date.today().isoformat()}')\n    r = requests.put(f'{BASE}/people/{pid}', auth=AUTH, headers=HDRS,\n                     json={'tags': new_tags}, timeout=15)\n    return r.status_code == 200\n\n\ndef log_convo(pid, line):\n    f = LOG_DIR / f'convo_{pid}.log'\n    with f.open('a') as fp:\n        fp.write(f'{datetime.datetime.now().isoformat()}  {line}\\n')\n\n\ndef process_contact(c):\n    pid = c['pid']\n    step = c['step']\n    msgs, jar = get_messages_for(pid)\n\n    last_out = find_last_our_outbound(msgs)\n    last_out_sent = last_out.get('sent', '') if last_out else None\n\n    # If step==0 and no outbound has happened yet, kick off Q1 now.\n    if step == 0 and not last_out:\n        body = QUESTIONS[0].format(first_name=c['firstName'])\n        sc, info = send_text(jar, pid, body, c['phone'])\n        if sc in (200, 201):\n            update_step(pid, c['tags'], 1)\n            log_convo(pid, f'BOT q1 sent: {body!r}')\n            print(f'  pid={pid} \u2192 sent Q1')\n        else:\n            log_convo(pid, f'BOT q1 FAIL: {sc} {info!r}')\n            print(f'  pid={pid} FAIL Q1: {sc}')\n        return\n\n    # If we've already sent the qualified close, do nothing.\n    if step > len(QUESTIONS):\n        return\n\n    # Was there a new inbound since our last outbound?\n    has_reply, reply = has_new_inbound_since(msgs, last_out_sent)\n    if not has_reply:\n        return  # waiting on lead\n\n    # Log their reply\n    log_convo(pid, f'LEAD reply (step={step}): {reply.get(\"message\", \"\")!r}')\n\n    # Advance: send next question, or qualify\n    if step < len(QUESTIONS):\n        body = QUESTIONS[step].format(first_name=c['firstName'])\n        sc, info = send_text(jar, pid, body, c['phone'])\n        if sc in (200, 201):\n            update_step(pid, c['tags'], step + 1)\n            log_convo(pid, f'BOT q{step+1} sent: {body!r}')\n            print(f'  pid={pid} \u2192 sent Q{step+1}')\n        else:\n            log_convo(pid, f'BOT q{step+1} FAIL: {sc} {info!r}')\n    else:\n        # All 3 questions answered. Send qualified message + tag.\n        body = QUALIFIED_REPLY.format(first_name=c['firstName'])\n        sc, info = send_text(jar, pid, body, c['phone'])\n        if sc in (200, 201):\n            mark_qualified(pid, c['tags'])\n            log_convo(pid, f'BOT qualified send: {body!r}')\n            log_convo(pid, 'BOT QUALIFIED. Conversation closed.')\n            print(f'  pid={pid} \u2192 QUALIFIED')\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(f'\\n=== chatbot responder run @ {datetime.datetime.now().isoformat()} ===')\n    contacts = find_active_contacts()\n    print(f'Active chatbot contacts: {len(contacts)}')\n    for c in contacts:\n        try:\n            process_contact(c)\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f'  pid={c[\"pid\"]} EXCEPTION: {type(e).__name__}: {e}')\n            log_convo(c['pid'], f'EXCEPTION: {type(e).__name__}: {e}')\n    print('Done.')\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n"
}

  "old_string": "def update_step(pid, current_tags, new_step):\n    new_tags = [t for t in (current_tags or []) if not t.startswith(STEP_TAG_PREFIX)]\n    new_tags.append(f'{STEP_TAG_PREFIX}{new_step}')\n    r = requests.put(f'{BASE}/people/{pid}', auth=AUTH, headers=HDRS,\n                     json={'tags': new_tags}, timeout=15)\n    return r.status_code == 200",
  "new_string": "def update_step(pid, current_tags, new_step, last_send_iso=None):\n    \"\"\"Set the chatbot step tag and, if given, the last-send-timestamp tag in ONE PUT.\"\"\"\n    new_tags = [t for t in (current_tags or [])\n                if not t.startswith(STEP_TAG_PREFIX)\n                and not t.startswith(LAST_SEND_TAG_PREFIX)]\n    new_tags.append(f'{STEP_TAG_PREFIX}{new_step}')\n    if last_send_iso:\n        new_tags.append(f'{LAST_SEND_TAG_PREFIX}{last_send_iso}')\n    r = requests.put(f'{BASE}/people/{pid}', auth=AUTH, headers=HDRS,\n                     json={'tags': new_tags}, timeout=15)\n    return r.status_code == 200\n\n\ndef get_last_send_iso(tags):\n    for t in (tags or []):\n        if t.startswith(LAST_SEND_TAG_PREFIX):\n            return t.split(':', 1)[1]\n    return None\n\n\ndef now_iso():\n    return datetime.datetime.utcnow().strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ')"
}

## assistant — 2026-05-16T21:37:24.161Z

Found it. **Not a bug — pool exhaustion from yesterday's dup damage.**

Today's data:
- Matthew: **72 outbound** (your manual sends + 20 from the 8:00 run + a few from chatbot + others)
- Adrianne: **1 outbound** (Jeanne Schramm at 10:31)

What happened: every Adrianne service fire today says **"Nothing due. Nobody needs a text right now."** — and that's true under current rules:

```
SPRIORITY contacts: 207
Stage filter: skipped 41 contacts in excluded stages
```

Of the remaining 166, **110+ have all 4 stage shadow tags (`_ai_text_d1+d3+d5+d7_spriority`)** from yesterday's dup-bug burn — the script thinks they finished their full 7-day sequence. The d1/d3/d5/d7 design is "touch a lead 4 times across 7 days, then done." Yesterday's bug crammed all 4 into 1 day; today the system thinks they're spent.

After excluded-stage + done-sequence + today's already-texted, the fresh eligible pool drops to ~5-10 per day. Matthew's 8:00 run drains it; Adrianne finds 0-1 stragglers from new Sierra tag-adds.

## Two fix options

**Option A — Reset the 110 burnt leads to re-enter their cycle today (immediate fix)**
Clear `_ai_text_d3/d5/d7_spriority` on the leads that got the bug yesterday. Keep `_ai_text_d1_spriority` (their d1 was a legit send). Now they're at "due for d3" — pool jumps back to ~110+ eligible. Today's remaining runs (Adrianne 14:30 just fired so next is 16:00 Matthew and 16:30 Adrianne) and tomorrow's runs pick them up. They each get 1 text per day max under the new dup-protection layers.

**Option B — Build a sequence-loop feature (long-term)**
After a lead finishes d7, wait N days (say 10), then strip the d3-d7 shadow tags so they re-enter the cycle. Means SPRIORITY leads get a fresh sequence every ~17 days perpetually, not just once.

I'd do **both**: Option A right now to fix today, Option B as a permanent feature so this dry-pool never happens again.

Want me to fire Option A immediately? Names of the burnt-yesterday leads include Hyacinth Roberts, Garry Dickerson, Rosemary Pena, Jill Cunningham, Lisa Belman, Maggie Avila and ~104 others.

  "file_path": "/home/matthew/automation/tiered_text_run.py",
  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"tiered_text_run.py \u2014 runs send_due_texts.py across multiple tag tiers in\npriority order, so once SPRIORITY is satisfied for the day we automatically\nfall back to 2026 Daily Search Done, then to Lead-stage cold pool.\n\nPer-day dedup is preserved by the `_texted_<date>` shadow tag that\nsend_due_texts.py writes after every send \u2014 a lead texted in tier 1 will be\nskipped in tier 2 and tier 3.\n\nEach tier has its own cap. Total possible texts per run is the SUM of caps,\nbut the today_stamp protection guarantees ONE TEXT PER LEAD PER DAY across\nall tiers.\n\nEnv passthrough:\n  FUB_DEBUG_PORT, FUB_FROM_PHONE set in systemd service file pick whether\n  this run is Matthew's (9222 / 725-600-6779) or Adrianne's (9223 / 725-777-1352).\n\"\"\"\nimport os\nimport sys\nimport subprocess\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent\n\n# (tag, skip_if_also_tagged, per_tier_cap)\nTIERS = [\n    ('SPRIORITY',               None,        20),\n    ('2026 Daily Search Done',  'SPRIORITY', 20),\n    # Lead-stage 14-day-no-contact tier added once the tag exists\n]\n\ndef main():\n    env = os.environ.copy()\n    for tag, skip, cap in TIERS:\n        args = [\n            str(ROOT / 'venv' / 'bin' / 'python'), '-u',\n            str(ROOT / 'send_due_texts.py'),\n            '--tag', tag,\n            '--cap', str(cap),\n        ]\n        if skip:\n            args.extend(['--skip-if-also-tagged', skip])\n        print(f'\\n========== Tier: {tag!r}  cap={cap} ==========')\n        r = subprocess.run(args, cwd=str(ROOT), env=env)\n        print(f'(tier exit code: {r.returncode})')\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n"
}
```

'lead', mobile, tags))\n        if len(batch) < 100:\n            break\n        offset += 100\n    return out\n\n\ndef main():\n    now = datetime.datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')\n    print(f'\\n=== speed_to_lead @ {now} ===')\n\n    cands = find_candidates()\n    print(f'Fresh SPRIORITY candidates (no {SPEED_TAG}): {len(cands)}')\n\n    if not cands:\n        print('Nothing to fire.')\n        return\n\n    cands = cands[:PER_RUN_CAP]\n    print(f'Firing up to {len(cands)} this run.')\n\n    jar = fetch_cookies(DEBUG_PORT)\n    today_stamp = f\"_texted_{datetime.date.today().isoformat()}\"\n    sent = 0\n    failed = 0\n\n    for pid, name, mobile, tags in cands:\n        # Reserve before send \u2014 write tags first\n        new_tags = list(tags) + [SPEED_TAG, today_stamp, '_ai_text_d1_spriority']\n        rr = requests.put(f'{BASE}/people/{pid}', json={'tags': new_tags},\n                          auth=AUTH, headers=HDRS, timeout=15)\n        if rr.status_code != 200:\n            failed += 1\n            print(f'  pid={pid} {name}: reservation FAIL {rr.status_code} \u2014 skipping')\n            continue\n\n        # Generate + send\n        gen, err = generate_message(jar, pid)\n        if err:\n            failed += 1\n            print(f'  pid={pid} {name}: gen FAIL {err}')\n            continue\n\n        r = send_text(jar, pid, gen['message'], mobile)\n        if r.status_code not in (200, 201):\n            failed += 1\n            print(f'  pid={pid} {name}: send FAIL {r.status_code} {r.text[:100]}')\n            continue\n\n        sent += 1\n        print(f'  pid={pid} {name}: SENT (gen #{gen.get(\"id\")})')\n        time.sleep(random.uniform(2, 4))   # pacing\n\n    print(f'\\nspeed_to_lead done: sent={sent} failed={failed}')\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n"
}
```

Got 2 min for a quick call today?\"\n\n\ndef fetch_cookies(port):\n    r = requests.get(f'http://localhost:{port}/json').json()\n    target = next((t for t in r if DOMAIN in t.get('url', '') and t.get('type') == 'page'), None) \\\n             or next((t for t in r if t.get('type') == 'page'), None)\n    ws = websocket.create_connection(target['webSocketDebuggerUrl'], timeout=10)\n    ws.send(json.dumps({'id': 1, 'method': 'Network.getAllCookies'}))\n    cookies = json.loads(ws.recv()).get('result', {}).get('cookies', [])\n    ws.close()\n    matching = [c for c in cookies if DOMAIN in c.get('domain', '')]\n    jar = requests.cookies.RequestsCookieJar()\n    for c in matching:\n        jar.set(c['name'], c['value'],\n                domain=c.get('domain', 'X-System': 'fub-spa', 'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest',\n        'Referer': f'https://{DOMAIN}/2/people/view/{person_id}',\n        'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0',\n    }\n\n\ndef fetch_candidates():\n    out = []\n    offset = 0\n    while offset < 1500:\n        r = requests.get(f'{BASE}/people',\n                         params={'tags': TARGET_TAG, 'limit': 100, 'offset': offset,\n                                 'fields': 'allFields'},\n                         auth=AUTH, headers=HDRS, timeout=30)\n        if r.status_code != 200:\n            break\n        batch = r.json().get('people', [])\n        if not batch:\n            break\n        for p in batch:\n            tags = p.get('tags') or []\n            if p.get('stageId') in EXCLUDED_STAGE_IDS:\n                continue\n            if '_chatbot_active' in tags or any(t.startswith('CHATBOT_QUALIFIED_') for t in tags):\n                continue\n            today_stamp = f\"_texted_{datetime.date.today().isoformat()}\"\n            if today_stamp in tags:\n                continue\n            last_received = p.get('lastReceivedText')\n            if last_received:\n                try:\n                    last_dt = datetime.datetime.strptime(last_received[:19], '%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S')\n                    if (datetime.datetime.utcnow() - last_dt).days < 7:\n                        continue\n                except Exception:\n                    pass\n            phones = p.get('phones') or []\n            mobile = next((re.sub(r'\\D', '', ph.get('value', '')) for ph in phones if ph.get('value')), None)\n            if not mobile:\n                continue\n            out.append({\n                'pid': p['id'],\n                'first_name': p.get('firstName') or (p.get('name', '').split() or ['there'])[0],\n                'mobile': mobile,\n                'tags': tags,\n            })\n        if len(batch) < 100:\n            break\n        offset += 100\n    return out\n\n\ndef send_text(jar, pid, msg, to_number):\n    payload = {'personId': int(pid), 'message': msg,\n               'toNumber': to_number, 'fromNumber': FROM_NUMBER_DIGITS}\n    r = requests.post(f'https://{DOMAIN}/api/v1/textMessages',\n                      cookies=jar, json=payload, headers=spa_headers(pid), timeout=20)\n    return r\n\n\ndef main():\n    now = datetime.datetime.now()\n    weekday = now.weekday()  # Mon=0, Sun=6\n    print(f'\\n=== morning_predial @ {now.strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S\")} (weekday={weekday}) ===')\n\n    # Defensive: even if the systemd timer day-of-week filter ever drifts,\n    # this script itself only fires on Mon/Wed/Fri.\n    if weekday not in (0, 2, 4):\n        print('Not a call day (Mon/Wed/Fri only). Skipping.')\n        return\n\n    cands = fetch_candidates()\n    print(f'Candidates: {len(cands)}  (cap {CAP})')\n    cands = cands[:CAP]\n    if not cands:\n        print('No one to warm-up text.')\n        return\n\n    jar = fetch_cookies(DEBUG_PORT)\n    today_stamp = f\"_texted_{datetime.date.today().isoformat()}\"\n    sent = failed = 0\n    for c in cands:\n        body = TEMPLATE.format(first_name=c['first_name'], agent=AGENT_NAME)\n        # Reserve first\n        new_tags = list(c['tags']) + ['_predial_sent', today_stamp]\n        rr = requests.put(f'{BASE}/people/{c[\"pid\"]}', json={'tags': new_tags},\n                          auth=AUTH, headers=HDRS, timeout=15)\n        if rr.status_code != 200:\n            failed += 1\n            print(f'  pid={c[\"pid\"]}: reserve FAIL {rr.status_code}')\n            continue\n        r = send_text(jar, c['pid'], body, c['mobile'])\n        if r.status_code in (200, 201):\n            sent += 1\n            print(f'  pid={c[\"pid\"]} {c[\"first_name\"]}: SENT')\n        else:\n            failed += 1\n            print(f'  pid={c[\"pid\"]}: send FAIL {r.status_code} {r.text[:100]}')\n        time.sleep(random.uniform(2, 4))\n\n    print(f'\\npredial done: sent={sent} failed={failed}')\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n"
}

## assistant — 2026-05-16T22:42:49.648Z

All four pieces armed. Here's the answer to your question + the full state.

## "Is there logic not to keep texting people who responded?"

**Now yes.** Patched `send_due_texts.py` to skip any lead whose `lastReceivedText` is within the last 7 days. Also skips `_chatbot_active` and `CHATBOT_QUALIFIED_*` leads. That filter applies to every text the sequence script ever picks.

## What I built this round

| | What | When it fires |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Reply suppression** in send_due_texts (replied <7d → skip) | Every text run |
| 2 | **Speed-to-lead** — polls FUB for new SPRIORITY tags, fires FUB-AI text within minutes | Every 5 min, 7am–9pm PT, **armed now (first fire in 2 min)** |
| 3 | **Pre-dial warmup** — "Got 2 min for a quick call today? — Matthew Dunlap" | Mon/Wed/Fri 8:30am PT, cap 30, **armed for Monday** |
| 4 | **Weekend email bump** — Phase 2 cap goes 500 → 750 per agent on Sat/Sun | Tonight's 20:00 phase2 fires at 750+750 = 1,500 emails |
| 5 | **SPRIORITY → 2026 tiered fallback** (Wave 2 from earlier) | Every text run automatically |
| 6 | **123 burnt leads reset** (Wave 1) | Already done |

## How speed-to-lead works in practice

1. Sierra detects engagement → existing Cloud Run pusher adds SPRIORITY tag to FUB
2. Within 5 minutes, `speed_to_lead.py` polls FUB, finds the freshly-tagged lead
3. Calls FUB's `generateMessage` to draft a personalized opener
4. Sends from your number (725-600-6779) and tags `_speedlead_sent` so it never fires twice on the same lead
5. The chatbot you tested handles the back-and-forth from there if they reply

Per-run cap is 8 (small batches — speed not volume). Cap is intentional so we never accidentally blast.

## What's coming in the next 4 hours

- **15:45 PT** — first speed-to-lead poll fires (if any fresh SPRIORITY tags exist, picks them up)
- **16:00 PT** — Matthew text run (now with reply suppression + 2026 fallback + 123-lead refreshed pool)
- **16:30 PT** — Adrianne text run (same)
- **18:00 PT** — daily dup-check email
- **20:00 PT** — Phase 2 weekend run (1,500 emails total)

## Predial — how it kicks off each day automatically

Mon/Wed/Fri at **8:30 AM PT**, `morning_predial.py` runs and texts up to 30 SPRIORITY-pool leads with "Hey {firstName}, it's Matthew Dunlap with Team Dunlap Realty in Vegas. Got 2 min for a quick call today?" — so when you start dialing later, your name is already on their phone.

Tag `PREDIAL_TAG` env var can change which pool it pulls from (default SPRIORITY). `PREDIAL_CAP` env var caps volume (default 30).

Speed-to-lead is **active** right now — next fire in ~90 seconds. You'll see action in `~/automation/logs/speed_to_lead.log` if any newly-SPRIORITY leads come in.

## assistant — 2026-05-16T23:24:28.893Z

All four steps are in place. Quick answer on your load-balancing question:

**How SPRIORITY load splits between you and Adrianne**

Three layers handle it:

1. **Lead ownership (FUB assignment)** — the rebalance script + round-robin assigner give each of you ~50% of the SPRIORITY contacts. New tags get round-robin'd ongoing.

2. **Bulk text sends (every 30 min, 7am–9pm)** — both numbers pull from the **same shared SPRIORITY pool**, not their assigned slice. The `_texted_<date>` shadow tag is what splits it: at 16:00 you fire 20, those leads get stamped, then at 16:30 Adrianne sees the stamped ones as ineligible and picks 20 different ones. So today's 123-lead reset pool will roughly halve between the two of you across the day. If the pool runs dry, `tiered_text_run.py` falls back to 2026 Daily Search Done.

3. **Speed-to-lead (every 5 min)** — now strict **per-lead alternation** via `.speed_lead_counter`. Lead 1 → Matthew, lead 2 → Adrianne, lead 3 → Matthew, regardless of who owns the lead in FUB. Both Chromes auto-launch via `ensure_chrome.sh` so a dead session doesn't bottleneck the other.

**What's live right now:**
- ✅ Reply suppression — both bulk + speed-to-lead skip anyone with `lastReceivedText` < 7 days old
- ✅ Speed-to-lead — enabled, alternating, fires every 5 min 7am–9pm
- ✅ Weekend email bump — Phase 2 fires 1500 emails tonight at 20:00 (750 each agent) to 2026 Daily Search Done
- ✅ Speed-to-lead number alternation — `.speed_lead_counter` persists across runs
- ✅ Predial — **disabled** as a timer, refactored to manual `READY_TO_CALL` tag model (tag leads, run `morning_predial.py`, delete tag after each call)
- ✅ Chatbot — your contact (pid 16198) re-armed with `_chatbot_active` and fresh step. Next 5-min poll will send Q1. Reply whenever you want; the bot picks up where it left off using `_chatbot_step:N` and `_chatbot_last_send:<iso>` tags.

The chatbot will keep texting you whenever you reply, and stay quiet when you don't. Have a good night.

## assistant — 2026-05-16T23:29:48.986Z

Yes — here's exactly how it works:

**Nightly run (11pm PT)** → `schedule_full_sequence.py` runs for both you and Adrianne. For each eligible lead, it builds three scheduled emails:

- **d1** → tomorrow 7:00am PT
- **d2** → day-after-tomorrow 7:00am PT
- **d5** → +5 days at 7:00am PT

**Two delivery paths, depending on `--via`:**

1. **FUB queue (default, `--via fub`)** — POSTs to FUB's `/api/v1/scheduledEmails` with `scheduleAt`. Email sits in FUB's outbound queue and FUB sends it at that timestamp. Shows up natively in the contact's FUB timeline, and FUB **auto-cancels** any scheduled email if the lead replies first.

2. **SendGrid (`--via sendgrid`)** — POSTs to SendGrid with `send_at` (unix timestamp). SendGrid holds it on their side and releases at the target time. Better inbox deliverability (warmed domain), but **capped at 72h out** — the d5 touch (~96h ahead) gets deferred to a future nightly that's closer in. Both paths drop a FUB note with subject + body so you can read the message right inside the contact.

**Phase 2 nightly (`phase2_reengage.py`, 20:00 PT)** uses `send_d1_emails_now.py` instead, which fires **immediately** via SendGrid (no queue, no `send_at`) — used for the big re-engagement blasts to 2026 Daily Search Done where we want them to land tonight, not tomorrow morning.

So the answer is: the d1/d2/d5 sequence drops them in queues (FUB or SendGrid) timestamped for 7am PT the morning of. The Phase 2 re-engagement blasts go out same-evening, not queued.

74	    """Construct the Sierra auto-login URL for a lead."""

76	    first_name = (assigned.get("agentUserFirstName") or "").lower().strip()
77	    subdomain = AGENT_SUBDOMAINS.get(first_name, DEFAULT_SUBDOMAIN)
78	    lead_id = lead.get("id")

```
1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
2	"""chatbot responder — beta v1 (template-based 3-question qualifier).
3	
4	Polls FUB for inbound texts from contacts tagged `_chatbot_active`.
5	Each new inbound advances the conversation by one question.
6	3 questions total, then the contact is tagged QUALIFIED for Matthew to call.
7	
8	Why no LLM tonight: Anthropic API key not yet configured, Ollama install
9	not yet done. The 3-question flow is goal-directed (per Matthew's
10	2026-05-15 instruction — find out where they live, why moving here, what
11	they're looking for) so a template flow accomplishes the same outcome.
12	
13	State per contact stored as FUB shadow tags `_chatbot_step:N`.
14	A conversation transcript log lives at chatbot/logs/convo_<pid>.log.
15	"""
16	import os
17	import sys
18	import json
19	import datetime
20	import time
21	import re
22	from pathlib import Path
23	import requests
24	import websocket
25	
26	ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]   # /home/matthew/automation
27	CHATBOT_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
28	LOG_DIR = CHATBOT_DIR / 'logs'
29	LOG_DIR.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
30	
31	for line in (ROOT / '.env').read_text().splitlines():
32	    if line.strip() and not line.startswith('#') and '=' in line:
33	        k, _, v = line.partition('=')
34	        os.environ.setdefault(k.strip(), v.strip())
35	
36	KEY = os.environ['FUB_API_KEY']
37	AUTH = (KEY, '')
38	HDRS = {'X-System': 'TeamDunlap-Chatbot'}
39	BASE = 'https://api.followupboss.com/v1'
40	DOMAIN = 'teamdunlaprealty.followupboss.com'
41	
42	ACTIVE_TAG = '_chatbot_active'
43	STEP_TAG_PREFIX = '_chatbot_step:'
44	LAST_SEND_TAG_PREFIX = '_chatbot_last_send:'
45	QUALIFIED_PREFIX = 'CHATBOT_QUALIFIED_'
46	
47	# We send from Matthew's number for chatbot tests, by default. Can override
48	# with FUB_FROM_PHONE env var for Adrianne.
49	FROM_NUMBER = os.environ.get('FUB_FROM_PHONE', '(725) 600-6779').replace('(', '').replace(')', '').replace('-', '').replace(' ', '')
50	DEBUG_PORT = int(os.environ.get('FUB_DEBUG_PORT', '9222'))
51	
52	QUESTIONS = [
53	    "Hi {first_name}, Matthew here. Quick question — where are you currently living?",
54	    "Thanks {first_name}. What's bringing you to look at Vegas — moving, investment, or second home?",
55	    "Got it. What kind of home are you looking for? Beds, baths, area, price range?",
56	]
57	QUALIFIED_REPLY = "Thanks {first_name} — great info. I'll give you a call to dig in deeper. What's a good time?"
58	
59	
60	def fetch_cookies(port):
61	    r = requests.get(f'http://localhost:{port}/json').json()
62	    target = next((t for t in r if DOMAIN in t.get('url', '') and t.get('type') == 'page'), None)
63	    if not target:
64	        target = next((t for t in r if t.get('type') == 'page'), None)
65	    ws = websocket.create_connection(target['webSocketDebuggerUrl'], timeout=10)
66	    ws.send(json.dumps({'id': 1, 'method': 'Network.getAllCookies'}))
67	    cookies = json.loads(ws.recv()).get('result', {}).get('cookies', [])
68	    ws.close()
69	    matching = [c for c in cookies if DOMAIN in c.get('domain', '')]
70	    jar = requests.cookies.RequestsCookieJar()
71	    for c in matching:
72	        jar.set(c['name'], c['value'],
73	                domain=c.get('domain', DOMAIN).lstrip('.'),
74	                path=c.get('path', '/'))
75	    return jar
76	
77	
78	def find_active_contacts():
79	    """Return [{pid, name, firstName, tags, phone, step}, ...] for contacts tagged _chatbot_active."""
80	    out = []
81	    offset = 0
82	    while offset < 200:
83	        r = requests.get(f'{BASE}/people',
84	                         params={'tags': ACTIVE_TAG, 'limit': 100, 'offset': offset, 'fields': 'allFields'},
85	                         auth=AUTH, headers=HDRS, timeout=20)
86	        if r.status_code != 200:
87	            break
88	        batch = r.json().get('people', []) or []
89	        if not batch:
90	            break
91	        for p in batch:
92	            tags = p.get('tags') or []
93	            step = 0
94	            for t in tags:
95	                if t.startswith(STEP_TAG_PREFIX):
96	                    try:
97	                        step = int(t.split(':', 1)[1])
98	                    except Exception:
99	                        pass
100	                    break
101	            phones = p.get('phones') or []
102	            mobile = next((ph.get('value') for ph in phones if ph.get('value')), None)
103	            if not mobile:
104	                continue
105	            out.append({
106	                'pid': p['id'],
107	                'name': p.get('name'),
108	                'firstName': p.get('firstName') or (p.get('name', '').split() or ['there'])[0],
109	                'tags': tags,
110	                'phone': re.sub(r'\D', '', mobile),
111	                'step': step,
112	            })
113	        if len(batch) < 100:
114	            break
115	        offset += 100
116	    return out
117	
118	
119	def get_messages_for(pid, limit=50):
120	    """Pull recent messages for this contact via SPA (full bodies). Returns most-recent first."""
121	    jar = fetch_cookies(DEBUG_PORT)
122	    headers = {'Accept': '*/*', 'X-System': 'fub-spa', 'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest',
123	               'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0',
124	               'Referer': f'https://{DOMAIN}/2/people/view/{pid}'}
125	    r = requests.get(f'https://{DOMAIN}/api/v1/textMessages',
126	                     params={'personId': pid, 'limit': limit},
127	                     cookies=jar, headers=headers, timeout=15)
128	    if r.status_code != 200:
129	        return [], jar
130	    msgs = r.json().get('textmessages', []) or []
131	    # Sort by sent ascending
132	    msgs.sort(key=lambda m: (m.get('sent') or ''))
133	    return msgs, jar
134	
135	
136	def has_new_inbound_since(msgs, since_iso):
137	    """True if any inbound message in msgs is after since_iso."""
138	    for m in msgs:
139	        if not m.get('isIncoming'):
140	            continue
141	        if since_iso is None or ((m.get('sent') or '') > since_iso):
142	            return True, m
143	    return False, None
144	
145	
146	def find_last_our_outbound(msgs):
147	    """Return the most-recent outbound from any of our numbers, or None."""
148	    OUR_NUMBERS = {'7256006779', '7257771352'}
149	    for m in reversed(msgs):
150	        if not m.get('isIncoming') and (m.get('fromNumber') or '').replace('+1', '').replace('(', '').replace(')', '').replace('-', '').replace(' ', '') in OUR_NUMBERS:
151	            return m
152	    return None
153	
154	
155	def send_text(jar, pid, body, to_phone):
156	    headers = {'Accept': '*/*', 'Content-Type': 'application/json',
157	               'X-System': 'fub-spa', 'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest',
158	               'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0',
159	               'Referer': f'https://{DOMAIN}/2/people/view/{pid}'}
160	    payload = {'personId': int(pid), 'message': body, 'toNumber': to_phone, 'fromNumber': FROM_NUMBER}
161	    r = requests.post(f'https://{DOMAIN}/api/v1/textMessages',
162	                      cookies=jar, headers=headers, json=payload, timeout=20)
163	    return r.status_code, (r.text[:200] if r.text else '')
164	
165	
166	def update_step(pid, current_tags, new_step, last_send_iso=None):
167	    """Set the chatbot step tag and, if given, the last-send-timestamp tag in ONE PUT."""
168	    new_tags = [t for t in (current_tags or [])
169	                if not t.startswith(STEP_TAG_PREFIX)
170	                and not t.startswith(LAST_SEND_TAG_PREFIX)]
171	    new_tags.append(f'{STEP_TAG_PREFIX}{new_step}')
172	    if last_send_iso:
173	        new_tags.append(f'{LAST_SEND_TAG_PREFIX}{last_send_iso}')
174	    r = requests.put(f'{BASE}/people/{pid}', auth=AUTH, headers=HDRS,
175	                     json={'tags': new_tags}, timeout=15)
176	    return r.status_code == 200
177	
178	
179	def get_last_send_iso(tags):
180	    for t in (tags or []):
181	        if t.startswith(LAST_SEND_TAG_PREFIX):
182	            return t.split(':', 1)[1]
183	    return None
184	
185	
186	def now_iso():
187	    return datetime.datetime.utcnow().strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ')
188	
189	
190	def mark_qualified(pid, current_tags):
191	    new_tags = [t for t in (current_tags or []) if t != ACTIVE_TAG and not t.startswith(STEP_TAG_PREFIX)]
192	    new_tags.append(f'{QUALIFIED_PREFIX}{datetime.date.today().isoformat()}')
193	    r = requests.put(f'{BASE}/people/{pid}', auth=AUTH, headers=HDRS,
194	                     json={'tags': new_tags}, timeout=15)
195	    return r.status_code == 200
196	
197	
198	def log_convo(pid, line):
199	    f = LOG_DIR / f'convo_{pid}.log'
200	    with f.open('a') as fp:
201	        fp.write(f'{datetime.datetime.now().isoformat()}  {line}\n')
202	
203	
204	def process_contact(c):
205	    pid = c['pid']
206	    step = c['step']
207	    last_send_iso = get_last_send_iso(c['tags'])
208	    msgs, jar = get_messages_for(pid)
209	
210	    # Step 0 = fresh start: ALWAYS send Q1, ignore any prior outbound history.
211	    if step == 0:
212	        body = QUESTIONS[0].format(first_name=c['firstName'])
213	        sc, info = send_text(jar, pid, body, c['phone'])
214	        if sc in (200, 201):
215	            ts = now_iso()
216	            update_step(pid, c['tags'], 1, ts)
217	            log_convo(pid, f'BOT q1 sent at {ts}: {body!r}')
218	            print(f'  pid={pid} → sent Q1')
219	        else:
220	            log_convo(pid, f'BOT q1 FAIL: {sc} {info!r}')
221	            print(f'  pid={pid} FAIL Q1: {sc} {info}')
222	        return
223	
224	    # If we've already sent the qualified close, do nothing.
225	    if step > len(QUESTIONS):
226	        return
227	
228	    if not last_send_iso:
229	        log_convo(pid, f'WARN step={step} but no {LAST_SEND_TAG_PREFIX} tag — cannot detect new inbound')
230	        return
231	
232	    # Was there an inbound from the lead AFTER our last bot-send?
233	    has_reply, reply = has_new_inbound_since(msgs, last_send_iso)
234	    if not has_reply:
235	        return  # still waiting on lead
236	
237	    log_convo(pid, f'LEAD reply (step={step}) at {reply.get("sent")}: {reply.get("message", "")!r}')
238	
239	    if step < len(QUESTIONS):
240	        body = QUESTIONS[step].format(first_name=c['firstName'])
241	        sc, info = send_text(jar, pid, body, c['phone'])
242	        if sc in (200, 201):
243	            ts = now_iso()
244	            update_step(pid, c['tags'], step + 1, ts)
245	            log_convo(pid, f'BOT q{step+1} sent at {ts}: {body!r}')
246	            print(f'  pid={pid} → sent Q{step+1}')
247	        else:
248	            log_convo(pid, f'BOT q{step+1} FAIL: {sc} {info!r}')
249	    else:
250	        body = QUALIFIED_REPLY.format(first_name=c['firstName'])
251	        sc, info = send_text(jar, pid, body, c['phone'])
252	        if sc in (200, 201):
253	            mark_qualified(pid, c['tags'])
254	            log_convo(pid, f'BOT qualified send: {body!r}')
255	            log_convo(pid, 'BOT QUALIFIED. Conversation closed.')
256	            print(f'  pid={pid} → QUALIFIED')
257	
258	
259	def main():
260	    print(f'\n=== chatbot responder run @ {datetime.datetime.now().isoformat()} ===')
261	    contacts = find_active_contacts()
262	    print(f'Active chatbot contacts: {len(contacts)}')
263	    for c in contacts:
264	        try:
265	            process_contact(c)
266	        except Exception as e:
267	            print(f'  pid={c["pid"]} EXCEPTION: {type(e).__name__}: {e}')
268	            log_convo(c['pid'], f'EXCEPTION: {type(e).__name__}: {e}')
269	    print('Done.')
270	
271	
272	if __name__ == '__main__':
273	    main()
274	
```

**Now the Sierra deep-link answer:**

Probed the Sierra API. The good news:
- `GET /savedSearches/find?leadId={id}` returns each lead's saved searches with `searchId`, criteria (price, beds, baths, city, etc.), and email-frequency settings.
- Your example lead Leilani (id 2443063, 11 visits) has 1 saved search: "General Las Vegas Property Search COPY" → `searchId: 7514662`, $300K–$2M, all property types, Vegas/Henderson/NLV/Boulder City.

The harder news:
- Sierra exposes **saved searches** (the recurring email-alert subscriptions) but NOT a "most-viewed" or "favorited listings" endpoint. I tried `/viewedListings/find`, `/favorites/find`, `/savedListings/find`, `/listings/find?savedByLeadId=...` — all 404.
- The auto-login URL we currently build (`https://www.thevegasagent.com/?userid={lead_id}&sentfrom=auto`) goes to the home page because that's all Sierra's API documents. There's no published `searchId` query-param to make it deep-link to a saved search.

**Two paths forward** (need your input on which):

1. **Reverse-engineer the deep-link** — log into one of your accounts in browser, click into a saved search, copy the URL pattern Sierra uses (likely something like `/listings/search/{searchId}` or `/account/savedSearches/{searchId}`), then I append `&userid={lead_id}&sentfrom=auto` to it. ~10 min to test once you grab a sample URL.

2. **"Most-viewed" path is dead** — Sierra's API doesn't expose viewed-listing history, so we can't auto-pick their top property without scraping or Sierra Support unlocking an endpoint. Saved-search deep-link is the realistic version of "their top search."

Easiest path: send me one URL from your Sierra portal when you click into a saved search (just the URL bar text), and I'll patch the auto-login builder tonight to deep-link there.
