1	"""
2	Sierra -> FUB auto-login URL sync (polling version)
3	
4	Runs on a schedule (e.g. every 30 min via GitHub Actions). Pulls all leads
5	from Sierra, constructs each lead's auto-login URL from the lead ID and
6	assigned agent, finds the matching FUB contact by email, and writes the
7	URL into a FUB custom field.
8	
9	URL format:  https://{agent}.thevegasagent.com/?userid={lead_id}&sentfrom=auto
10	
11	Required env vars:
12	    SIERRA_API_KEY               Sierra Interactive Direct API key
13	    FUB_API_KEY                  Follow Up Boss API key (account-owner key)
14	    FUB_CUSTOM_FIELD             FUB custom field API name (e.g. customSierraLoginURL)
15	    SIERRA_ORIGINATING_SYSTEM    (optional) Name to send as Sierra-OriginatingSystemName
16	"""
17	
18	import os
19	import time
20	import requests
21	from pathlib import Path
22	
23	
24	def _load_env_file():
25	    """Load .env from the script's folder if it exists. No-op in production
26	    (GitHub Actions / Render) where env vars are already set."""
27	    env_path = Path(__file__).parent / ".env"
28	    if not env_path.exists():
29	        return
30	    for line in env_path.read_text().splitlines():
31	        line = line.strip()
32	        if not line or line.startswith("#") or "=" not in line:
33	            continue
34	        key, _, value = line.partition("=")
35	        key = key.strip()
36	        value = value.strip().strip('"').strip("'")
37	        # Only set if not already in environment - real env vars take priority
38	        if key and key not in os.environ:
39	            os.environ[key] = value
40	
41	
42	_load_env_file()
43	
44	SIERRA_API_KEY = os.environ["SIERRA_API_KEY"]
45	FUB_API_KEY = os.environ["FUB_API_KEY"]
46	FUB_CUSTOM_FIELD = os.environ.get("FUB_CUSTOM_FIELD", "customSierraLoginURL")
47	SIERRA_ORIGINATING_SYSTEM = os.environ.get(
48	    "SIERRA_ORIGINATING_SYSTEM", "FUB-AutoLogin-Sync"
49	)
50	
51	SIERRA_BASE = "https://api.sierrainteractivedev.com"
52	FUB_BASE = "https://api.followupboss.com/v1"
53	
54	SIERRA_HEADERS = {
55	    "Sierra-ApiKey": SIERRA_API_KEY,
56	    "Sierra-OriginatingSystemName": SIERRA_ORIGINATING_SYSTEM,
57	}
58	
59	# Per-agent subdomain map. Lookup is by lowercased first name.
60	# Matthew is the team leader, so his URL is the main team site (www).
61	AGENT_SUBDOMAINS = {
62	    "matthew": "www",
63	    "adrianne": "adrianne",
64	}
65	# Fallback subdomain when a lead's agent isn't in the map (or has no agent).
66	DEFAULT_SUBDOMAIN = "www"
67	SITE_DOMAIN = "thevegasagent.com"
68	
69	# FUB rate limit is 1000 requests / 10 minutes per API key.
70	SLEEP_BETWEEN_WRITES = 0.7
71	
72	
73	def build_login_url(lead):
74	    """Construct the Sierra auto-login URL for a lead."""
75	    assigned = lead.get("assignedTo") or {}
76	    first_name = (assigned.get("agentUserFirstName") or "").lower().strip()
77	    subdomain = AGENT_SUBDOMAINS.get(first_name, DEFAULT_SUBDOMAIN)
78	    lead_id = lead.get("id")
79	    if not lead_id:
80	        return None
81	    return f"https://{subdomain}.{SITE_DOMAIN}/?userid={lead_id}&sentfrom=auto"
82	
83	
84	def get_sierra_leads(page=1, page_size=100):
85	    """Pull a page of leads from Sierra.
86	
87	    NOTE: Sierra's API expects 'pageNumber', not 'page'. Using 'page' is
88	    silently ignored and always returns page 1.
89	    """
90	    r = requests.get(
91	        f"{SIERRA_BASE}/leads/find",
92	        headers=SIERRA_HEADERS,
93	        params={"pageNumber": page, "pageSize": page_size},
94	        timeout=30,
95	    )
96	    r.raise_for_status()
97	    return r.json().get("data", {}).get("leads", [])
98	
99	
100	def get_sierra_total_pages(page_size=100):
101	    """Find the total number of pages in /leads/find."""
102	    r = requests.get(
103	        f"{SIERRA_BASE}/leads/find",
104	        headers=SIERRA_HEADERS,
105	        params={"pageNumber": 1, "pageSize": page_size},
106	        timeout=30,
107	    )
108	    r.raise_for_status()
109	    return r.json().get("data", {}).get("totalPages", 1)
110	
111	
112	def sync_recent(num_recent_pages=2):
113	    """Incremental sync - only checks the last few pages of Sierra
114	    (where the newest leads live, since pagination is ascending by lead ID).
115	    Designed for high-frequency cron runs."""
116	    from datetime import datetime
117	    start = datetime.now()
118	    print(f"[{start:%H:%M:%S}] Starting INCREMENTAL sync (last {num_recent_pages} pages)")
119	
120	    total_pages = get_sierra_total_pages()
121	    print(f"  Sierra reports {total_pages} total pages.")
122	    pages_to_check = list(range(max(1, total_pages - num_recent_pages + 1), total_pages + 1))
123	    print(f"  Checking pages: {pages_to_check}")
124	
125	    updated = 0
126	    skipped_already_set = 0
127	    skipped_no_match = 0
128	    skipped_missing_data = 0
129	
130	    for p in pages_to_check:
131	        leads = get_sierra_leads(page=p)
132	        for lead in leads:
133	            email = lead.get("email")
134	            login_url = build_login_url(lead)
135	            if not email or not login_url:
136	                skipped_missing_data += 1
137	                continue
138	            person = find_fub_person(email)
139	            if not person:
140	                skipped_no_match += 1
141	                continue
142	            if person.get(FUB_CUSTOM_FIELD) == login_url:
143	                skipped_already_set += 1
144	                continue
145	            if update_fub_person(person["id"], login_url, email_for_logs=email):
146	                updated += 1
147	            time.sleep(SLEEP_BETWEEN_WRITES)
148	
149	    elapsed = (datetime.now() - start).total_seconds()
150	    print(
151	        f"\n=== Incremental sync done ===\n"
152	        f"Updated: {updated}\n"
153	        f"Already set: {skipped_already_set}\n"
154	        f"No FUB match: {skipped_no_match}\n"
155	        f"Missing data: {skipped_missing_data}\n"
156	        f"Elapsed: {int(elapsed//60)}m{int(elapsed%60)}s"
157	    )
158	
159	
160	def _fub_request_with_retry(method, url, **kwargs):
161	    """Make a FUB request with automatic 429 retry (up to 3 attempts)."""
162	    for attempt in range(3):
163	        r = requests.request(method, url, auth=(FUB_API_KEY, ""), timeout=30, **kwargs)
164	        if r.status_code == 429:
165	            wait = int(r.headers.get("Retry-After", 15))
166	            print(f"  [429 rate limited, waiting {wait}s before retry {attempt+1}/3]")
167	            time.sleep(wait)
168	            continue
169	        return r
170	    return r  # final response, even if still 429
171	
172	
173	def find_fub_person(email):
174	    """Find a FUB person by email. Returns dict or None."""
175	    r = _fub_request_with_retry(
176	        "GET",
177	        f"{FUB_BASE}/people",
178	        params={"email": email, "fields": "allFields"},
179	    )
180	    if r.status_code != 200:
181	        print(f"  FUB find failed for {email}: HTTP {r.status_code} {r.text[:120]}")
182	        return None
183	    people = r.json().get("people", [])
184	    return people[0] if people else None
185	
186	
187	def update_fub_person(person_id, login_url, email_for_logs=""):
188	    """Push the Sierra login URL into the FUB custom field."""
189	    r = _fub_request_with_retry(
190	        "PUT",
191	        f"{FUB_BASE}/people/{person_id}",
192	        json={FUB_CUSTOM_FIELD: login_url},
193	    )
194	    if r.status_code != 200:
195	        print(f"  FUB update failed for {email_for_logs} (id={person_id}): "
196	              f"HTTP {r.status_code} {r.text[:200]}")
197	        return False
198	    return True
199	
200	
201	def sync(limit=None, dry_run=False):
202	    """
203	    Run the sync.
204	
205	    Args:
206	        limit: Optional. If set, stop after processing this many leads.
207	        dry_run: If True, don't actually write to FUB - just print what would happen.
208	    """
209	    from datetime import datetime
210	    start = datetime.now()
211	    page = 1
212	    processed = 0
213	    updated = 0
214	    skipped_no_match = 0
215	    skipped_already_set = 0
216	    skipped_missing_data = 0
217	
218	    print(f"[{start:%H:%M:%S}] Starting sync ({'DRY RUN' if dry_run else 'LIVE'})")
219	
220	    while True:
221	        leads = get_sierra_leads(page=page)
222	        if not leads:
223	            break
224	
225	        for lead in leads:
226	            email = lead.get("email")
227	            login_url = build_login_url(lead)
228	
229	            if not email or not login_url:
230	                skipped_missing_data += 1
231	                continue
232	
233	            person = find_fub_person(email)
234	            if not person:
235	                skipped_no_match += 1
236	                continue
237	
238	            if person.get(FUB_CUSTOM_FIELD) == login_url:
239	                skipped_already_set += 1
240	            elif dry_run:
241	                print(f"[DRY RUN] would set {email} -> {login_url}")
242	                updated += 1
243	            else:
244	                if update_fub_person(person["id"], login_url, email_for_logs=email):
245	                    updated += 1
246	                time.sleep(SLEEP_BETWEEN_WRITES)
247	
248	            processed += 1
249	            if limit and processed >= limit:
250	                _print_summary(updated, skipped_already_set, skipped_no_match,
251	                               skipped_missing_data, dry_run, start)
252	                return
253	
254	        # End-of-page progress report
255	        elapsed = (datetime.now() - start).total_seconds()
256	        print(
257	            f"[{datetime.now():%H:%M:%S}] page {page} done. "
258	            f"updated={updated} matched={updated+skipped_already_set} "
259	            f"no_match={skipped_no_match} no_data={skipped_missing_data} "
260	            f"elapsed={int(elapsed//60)}m{int(elapsed%60)}s"
261	        )
262	        page += 1
263	
264	    _print_summary(updated, skipped_already_set, skipped_no_match,
265	                   skipped_missing_data, dry_run, start)
266	
267	
268	def _print_summary(updated, already_set, no_match, missing, dry_run, start=None):
269	    label = "Would update" if dry_run else "Updated"
270	    line = (
271	        f"\n=== Summary ===\n"
272	        f"{label}: {updated}\n"
273	        f"Already set (skipped): {already_set}\n"
274	        f"No FUB match: {no_match}\n"
275	        f"Missing data (skipped): {missing}"
276	    )
277	    if start is not None:
278	        from datetime import datetime
279	        elapsed = (datetime.now() - start).total_seconds()
280	        line += f"\nTotal time: {int(elapsed//60)}m{int(elapsed%60)}s"
281	    print(line)
282	
283	
284	if __name__ == "__main__":
285	    import sys
286	    args = sys.argv[1:]
287	    dry_run = "--dry-run" in args
288	    if "--recent" in args:
289	        # Incremental mode - just check the last few pages where new leads live
290	        pages = 2
291	        for a in args:
292	            if a.startswith("--recent-pages="):
293	                pages = int(a.split("=", 1)[1])
294	        sync_recent(num_recent_pages=pages)
295	    else:
296	        limit = None
297	        for a in args:
298	            if a.startswith("--limit="):
299	                limit = int(a.split("=", 1)[1])
300	        sync(limit=limit, dry_run=dry_run)
301

  "new_string": "def mark_qualified(pid, current_tags):\n    new_tags = [t for t in (current_tags or []) if t != ACTIVE_TAG and not t.startswith(STEP_TAG_PREFIX)]\n    new_tags.append(f'{QUALIFIED_PREFIX}{datetime.date.today().isoformat()}')\n    r = requests.put(f'{BASE}/people/{pid}', auth=AUTH, headers=HDRS,\n                     json={'tags': new_tags}, timeout=15)\n    return r.status_code == 200\n\n\ndef reset_for_testing(pid, current_tags):\n    \"\"\"Strip chatbot state and re-arm with _chatbot_active so next poll resends Q1.\"\"\"\n    new_tags = [t for t in (current_tags or [])\n                if not (t.startswith(STEP_TAG_PREFIX)\n                        or t.startswith(LAST_SEND_TAG_PREFIX)\n                        or t.startswith(QUALIFIED_PREFIX))]\n    if ACTIVE_TAG not in new_tags:\n        new_tags.append(ACTIVE_TAG)\n    r = requests.put(f'{BASE}/people/{pid}', auth=AUTH, headers=HDRS,\n                     json={'tags': new_tags}, timeout=15)\n    return r.status_code == 200"

Both shipped. Summary:

**Sierra deep-link → live in `sierra_fub_sync.py`**
- New `get_top_saved_search_id()` pulls each lead's saved searches and picks the one Sierra is actively emailing (`sendEmailAlert == "On"`), tie-break on highest frequency.
- `build_login_url()` now returns: `https://{agent}.thevegasagent.com/property-search/results/?searchtype=2&searchid={searchId}&userid={lead_id}&sentfrom=auto`
- Falls back to the old home-page URL if the lead has no saved searches.
- Smoke-tested against Victor → byte-identical to the URL you sent.

**Heads-up:** the sync isn't on a systemd timer on this Linux box — looks like it runs from a separate GitHub repo. Once that fires (or whenever you run `./venv/bin/python sierra_fub_sync.py` manually), the deep-link version starts replacing the stored URL on the leads it touches. For a one-shot full-DB backfill of all ~10k leads, run it with no flags; takes a while. Want me to schedule it on this box too, or trigger a manual backfill now?

**Chatbot — 3-question flow with Zoom close:**
- Q1: *"What's bringing you to Vegas — moving, investment, or second home?"*
- Q2: *"What kind of home are you looking for? Beds, baths, area, price range?"*
- Q3: *"When are you hoping to make a move — next few weeks, few months, or just exploring?"*
- Close: *"Perfect {name} — let's hop on a quick 15-min Zoom so I can pull exactly what you want. Grab a slot here: {ZOOM_LINK}"*

**ZOOM_LINK** isn't set yet — until you add `ZOOM_LINK=https://...` to `/home/matthew/automation/.env`, the close message will literally include `[ZOOM_LINK_NOT_SET]` (visible placeholder so we don't fake-look-good).

**Test mode on you (pid 16198):** after the Zoom close fires, the bot auto-resets your contact back to step 0 instead of marking QUALIFIED. So you can keep iterating forever without re-tagging. Your contact is already reset and re-armed — next 5-min poll sends the new Q1.

Production leads still get marked `CHATBOT_QUALIFIED_<date>` and drop out of the chatbot pool when they hit the close. Reply whenever you want.

1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
2	"""Pull all FUB text conversations with FULL BODIES via the cookie-authed
3	SPA endpoint. Saves to chatbot/corpus/fub_texts_corpus.jsonl (one JSON per line).
4	
5	The public REST API hides bodies ("* Body is hidden for privacy reasons *").
6	The SPA endpoint at teamdunlaprealty.followupboss.com/api/v1/textMessages
7	returns real bodies because that's what the FUB UI uses.
8	
9	Usage:
10	    python3 pull_fub_text_corpus.py                 # all texts both numbers
11	    python3 pull_fub_text_corpus.py --max 500       # stop after N
12	    python3 pull_fub_text_corpus.py --since 2024    # only sent after YYYY
13	"""
14	import os
15	import sys
16	import json
17	import time
18	import websocket
19	from pathlib import Path
20	import requests
21	
22	ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]   # /home/matthew/automation
23	for line in (ROOT / '.env').read_text().splitlines():
24	    if line.strip() and not line.startswith('#') and '=' in line:
25	        k, _, v = line.partition('=')
26	        os.environ.setdefault(k.strip(), v.strip())
27	
28	DOMAIN = 'teamdunlaprealty.followupboss.com'
29	NUMBERS = {'7256006779': 'Matthew', '7257771352': 'Adrianne'}
30	OUT_FILE = Path(__file__).parent / 'fub_texts_corpus.jsonl'
31	
32	PORT = 9222
33	SINCE = None
34	MAX_TOTAL = 0
35	for i, a in enumerate(sys.argv):
36	    if a == '--max' and i + 1 < len(sys.argv):
37	        MAX_TOTAL = int(sys.argv[i + 1])
38	    elif a == '--since' and i + 1 < len(sys.argv):
39	        SINCE = sys.argv[i + 1]   # ISO date prefix
40	
41	
42	def fetch_cookies(port):
43	    r = requests.get(f'http://localhost:{port}/json').json()
44	    target = next((t for t in r if DOMAIN in t.get('url', '') and t.get('type') == 'page'), None)
45	    if not target:
46	        target = next((t for t in r if t.get('type') == 'page'), None)
47	    ws = websocket.create_connection(target['webSocketDebuggerUrl'], timeout=10)
48	    ws.send(json.dumps({'id': 1, 'method': 'Network.getAllCookies'}))
49	    cookies = json.loads(ws.recv()).get('result', {}).get('cookies', [])
50	    ws.close()
51	    matching = [c for c in cookies if DOMAIN in c.get('domain', '')]
52	    jar = requests.cookies.RequestsCookieJar()
53	    for c in matching:
54	        jar.set(c['name'], c['value'],
55	                domain=c.get('domain', DOMAIN).lstrip('.'),
56	                path=c.get('path', '/'))
57	    return jar
58	
59	
60	def main():
61	    jar = fetch_cookies(PORT)
62	    headers = {'Accept': '*/*', 'X-System': 'fub-spa',
63	               'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest',
64	               'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0'}
65	
66	    out_f = OUT_FILE.open('w')
67	    total = 0
68	    # Walk each direction × each of our numbers
69	    for our_number, label in NUMBERS.items():
70	        for direction in ('fromNumber', 'toNumber'):
71	            params = {direction: our_number, 'limit': 100}
72	            url = f'https://{DOMAIN}/api/v1/textMessages'
73	            while True:
74	                r = requests.get(url, params=params, cookies=jar, headers=headers, timeout=30)
75	                if r.status_code != 200:
76	                    print(f'  HTTP {r.status_code} on {direction}={our_number}: {r.text[:200]}')
77	                    break
78	                data = r.json()
79	                batch = data.get('textmessages', [])
80	                if not batch:
81	                    break
82	                for m in batch:
83	                    sent = m.get('sent') or ''
84	                    if SINCE and sent < SINCE:
85	                        continue
86	                    body = m.get('message', '')
87	                    if not body or '* Body is hidden' in body:
88	                        continue
89	                    rec = {
90	                        'id': m.get('id'),
91	                        'personId': m.get('personId'),
92	                        'name': m.get('name'),
93	                        'isIncoming': m.get('isIncoming'),
94	                        'sent': sent,
95	                        'fromNumber': m.get('fromNumber'),
96	                        'toNumber': m.get('toNumber'),
97	                        'message': body,
98	                        'ourSide': label,
99	                    }
100	                    out_f.write(json.dumps(rec) + '\n')
101	                    total += 1
102	                if MAX_TOTAL and total >= MAX_TOTAL:
103	                    break
104	                meta = data.get('_metadata', {})
105	                next_link = meta.get('nextLink')
106	                if not next_link:
107	                    break
108	                url = next_link
109	                params = {}  # nextLink encodes pagination
110	                time.sleep(0.15)
111	                if total % 500 == 0:
112	                    print(f'  pulled {total} so far ({direction}={our_number})')
113	                if MAX_TOTAL and total >= MAX_TOTAL:
114	                    break
115	            if MAX_TOTAL and total >= MAX_TOTAL:
116	                break
117	        if MAX_TOTAL and total >= MAX_TOTAL:
118	            break
119	
120	    out_f.close()
121	    print(f'\nDone. Wrote {total} text records to {OUT_FILE}')
122	
123	
124	if __name__ == '__main__':
125	    main()
126

Grab a slot here: \" + ZOOM_LINK,\n    \"Awesome {first_name}. Best next step: 15 mins on Zoom so I can show you the homes that fit. Book a time: \" + ZOOM_LINK,\n    \"Love it {first_name}. Bot acks the note and waits.\nTRAINING_PATTERN = re.compile(r'\\(\\s*for\\s+claude\\s*:\\s*(.+?)\\s*\\)', re.IGNORECASE | re.DOTALL)\nTRAINING_NOTES_FILE = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / 'training_notes.md'\n\n\ndef get_question(step_index, first_name):\n    \"\"\"Pick a random variant of the question for this step.\"\"\"\n    variants = QUESTIONS[step_index]\n    return random.choice(variants).format(first_name=first_name)\n\n\ndef get_close(first_name):\n    return random.choice(QUALIFIED_REPLY_VARIANTS).format(first_name=first_name)\n\n\ndef extract_training_note(message_body):\n    \"\"\"Return (note, stripped_message) if a (for claude: ...) directive exists, else (None, message_body).\"\"\"\n    if not message_body:\n        return None, message_body\n    m = TRAINING_PATTERN.search(message_body)\n    if not m:\n        return None, message_body\n    note = m.group(1).strip()\n    stripped = TRAINING_PATTERN.sub('', message_body).strip()\n    return note, stripped\n\n\ndef append_training_note(pid, first_name, note, full_reply):\n    \"\"\"Save a training note to a markdown log Matthew can review.\"\"\"\n    TRAINING_NOTES_FILE.parent.mkdir(exist_ok=True)\n    ts = datetime.datetime.now().isoformat(timespec='seconds')\n    entry = (\n        f\"\\n## {ts}  pid={pid}  ({first_name})\\n\"\n        f\"- **Note**: {note}\\n\"\n        f\"- **In reply**: {full_reply!r}\\n\"\n    )\n    with TRAINING_NOTES_FILE.open('a') as f:\n        f.write(entry)"

## assistant — 2026-05-17T01:19:23.966Z

All four items addressed. Here's the wrap:

**1. Dup race fixed (the 6pm bug)**
Root cause was a 5-second race between `speed_to_lead.py` (fired at 16:35:00) and `send_due_texts.py` (Adrianne's bulk loop reached Neva at 16:35:05). Speed-to-lead's reservation PUT hadn't propagated through FUB's read-after-write lag before Adrianne's just-in-time refetch read stale tags. Both sent.

Shared lockfile (`/home/matthew/automation/.texting_lock.pid`) now wraps both scripts via `fcntl.LOCK_EX | LOCK_NB`. If bulk is running, speed-to-lead exits cleanly and retries on the next 5-min poll. No more cross-script races possible.

**2. Chatbot — 5 prompts, variety, in-band training**
- 5 question steps (was 3): why-Vegas → criteria → timing → financing → open-ended → Zoom close.
- Each step has 3 phrasings, picked randomly per send. So you'll see different openers across test sessions.
- 3 Zoom-close variants, also randomized.
- **`(for claude: ...)` directive** — drop that inside any reply and the bot:
  - Saves the note to `/home/matthew/automation/chatbot/training_notes.md` with timestamp + pid + full reply context
  - Texts back: *"Got it — noted: 'your note here'. Continuing."*
  - Does NOT advance the question step — waits for your real answer next
  - Example: *"yeah I'm cash (for claude: more casual on the financing question please)"* → note saved, no step advance, your next reply moves the flow forward
- Test-mode reset on you (pid 16198) unchanged — bot will loop forever on your contact.

**3. Ylopo AI corpus — what we actually have**
Confirmed 10,780 messages with FULL bodies in `chatbot/corpus/fub_texts_corpus.jsonl`, range 2025-08-12 → today. Sampled FUB's 10 oldest leads (all created 2021-03-11) and only **1 of 10** had any text history at all (Anthony Higgins, 2 texts from late 2025). 

That suggests the Ylopo-era engagement was email-driven, not SMS — your text corpus is essentially everything you and Adrianne have ever texted from those two numbers. The FUB UI tabs you're seeing (Inbox / Assigned / Sent / Closed) are filter views of the same data the SPA endpoint returns, not separate stores.

If you can name ONE lead where you can see a Ylopo AI text in the FUB inbox and we're missing it, I'll pull that lead's full thread and figure out what filter is hiding it. Otherwise the corpus is complete.

**4. Status of your active test conversation**
Your contact (pid 16198) was already reset earlier — next 5-min poll sends a random Q1 variant. Reply normally and the bot walks 5 steps then loops. Drop `(for claude: ...)` whenever you want to teach it something on the fly.
